SET statement_timeout = 0;
SET lock_timeout = 0;
SET idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = 0;
SET transaction_timeout = 0;
SET client_encoding = 'UTF8';
SET standard_conforming_strings = on;
SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', '', false);
SET check_function_bodies = false;
SET xmloption = content;
SET client_min_messages = warning;
SET row_security = off;

--
-- Name: public; Type: SCHEMA; Schema: -; Owner: -
--



--
-- Name: SCHEMA public; Type: COMMENT; Schema: -; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON SCHEMA public IS 'standard public schema';


--
-- Name: donation_program_ok(text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.donation_program_ok(pid text) RETURNS boolean
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'public'
    AS $$ select exists (select 1 from programs where id = pid and data->>'status' is distinct from 'selesai') $$;


--
-- Name: pending_donor_count(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.pending_donor_count() RETURNS integer
    LANGUAGE sql SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'public'
    AS $$ select count(*)::int from donors where data->>'status' = 'pending' $$;


--
-- Name: rls_auto_enable(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--



--
-- Name: submit_donation(jsonb); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.submit_donation(p jsonb) RETURNS jsonb
    LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'public'
    AS $_$
declare
  v_id     text := p->>'id';
  v_prog   text := p->>'program';
  v_name   text := btrim(coalesce(p->>'name', ''));
  v_amount numeric;
  v_auto   boolean;
  v_status text;
  v_ip     text;
  v_hdr    json := nullif(current_setting('request.headers', true), '')::json;
  v_row    jsonb;
begin
  -- validasi (sama ketatnya dengan hardening.sql)
  if v_id is null or v_id !~ '^donasi-[a-z0-9-]{3,60}$' then raise exception 'id donasi tidak valid'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p->'amount') is distinct from 'number' then raise exception 'nominal tidak valid'; end if;
  v_amount := (p->>'amount')::numeric;
  if v_amount not between 10000 and 100000000 then raise exception 'nominal di luar batas'; end if;
  if char_length(v_name) not between 3 and 80 then raise exception 'nama tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(p->>'method', '') not in ('BSI', 'QRIS', 'E-wallet', 'Tunai', 'Lainnya') then raise exception 'metode tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(jsonb_typeof(p->'anon'), 'boolean') <> 'boolean' then raise exception 'data tidak valid'; end if;
  if not donation_program_ok(v_prog) then raise exception 'program tidak tersedia'; end if;

  -- rate limit: maks 200 donasi/jam untuk seluruh situs dan 20/jam per alamat IP
  v_ip := nullif(btrim(coalesce(v_hdr->>'cf-connecting-ip', split_part(coalesce(v_hdr->>'x-forwarded-for', ''), ',', 1))), '');
  if (select count(*) from donors where created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 200 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;
  if v_ip is not null and (select count(*) from donors where ip = v_ip and created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 20 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi dari perangkat ini, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;

  -- MODE DEMO: bila settings "bayar" berisi auto=true, donasi langsung terverifikasi
  select coalesce((data->>'auto')::boolean, false) into v_auto from settings where key = 'bayar';
  v_status := case when coalesce(v_auto, false) then 'verified' else 'pending' end;

  v_row := jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_id, 'name', v_name, 'program', v_prog, 'amount', v_amount, 'method', p->>'method',
    'date', to_char(now() at time zone 'Asia/Jakarta', 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
    'status', v_status, 'anon', coalesce((p->>'anon')::boolean, false));
  insert into donors (id, data, ip) values (v_id, v_row, v_ip);

  if v_status = 'verified' then
    update programs set data = jsonb_set(jsonb_set(data, '{collected}', to_jsonb(coalesce((data->>'collected')::numeric, 0) + v_amount)),
                                         '{donors}', to_jsonb(coalesce((data->>'donors')::int, 0) + 1))
     where id = v_prog;
  end if;
  return v_row;
end $_$;


SET default_tablespace = '';

SET default_table_access_method = heap;

CREATE TABLE public.activities (
    pos bigint NOT NULL,
    id text NOT NULL,
    data jsonb NOT NULL

ALTER TABLE public.activities ALTER COLUMN pos ADD GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY (
    SEQUENCE NAME public.activities_pos_seq
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO MINVALUE

    CACHE 1
);

--
-- Name: public_donors; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.public_donors AS
 SELECT id,
    pos,
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(((data ->> 'anon'::text))::boolean, false) THEN jsonb_set(data, '{name}'::text[], '"Hamba Allah"'::jsonb)
            ELSE data
        END AS data
   FROM public.donors
  WHERE ((data ->> 'status'::text) IS DISTINCT FROM 'pending'::text);


--
-- Name: settings; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.settings (
    key text NOT NULL,
    data jsonb NOT NULL
);

=== README.md ===
# Website Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah (Vue 3 + Supabase)

Stack: Vue 3, Vite, Tailwind CSS 4, Headless UI, Heroicons, Chart.js, Leaflet, Supabase.

## 1. Siapkan Supabase
1. Buat project di https://supabase.com.
2. SQL Editor: jalankan `supabase/schema.sql`, `supabase/seed.sql` (data awal), lalu `supabase/donasi-publik.sql` (donasi dari pengunjung + realtime) `supabase/media.sql` (penyimpanan gambar), `supabase/hardening.sql` (aturan lebih ketat + batas ukuran gambar), lalu `supabase/donasi-otomatis.sql` dan `supabase/donasi-konfirmasi.sql` (donasi lewat fungsi database, selalu menunggu verifikasi).
3. Authentication > Users > **Add user**: isi email + password admin (centang Auto Confirm).
4. Authentication > Sign In / Providers > **matikan "Allow new users to sign up"** (agar hanya Anda yang bisa jadi admin).
5. Project Settings > API: salin **Project URL** dan **anon public key**.

## 2. Jalankan lokal
    cp .env.example .env     # isi URL dan anon key
    npm install
    npm run dev              # situs http://localhost:5173  |  admin http://localhost:5173/#admin

## 3. Deploy ke Vercel
Push ke GitHub, import di Vercel (framework: Vite). Di Settings > Environment Variables isi
`VITE_SUPABASE_URL` dan `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, lalu deploy.

## Keamanan
- Anon key memang publik; yang melindungi data adalah RLS di `schema.sql`: pengunjung hanya bisa
  membaca data publik, sedangkan ubah/hapus hanya untuk admin yang login.
- Tabel `donors` tidak dibuka ke publik; situs membaca view `public_donors` (hanya donasi
  terverifikasi, nama anonim disamarkan).
- Jangan pernah memakai `service_role` key di kode front end.

## Alur donasi
Pengunjung memilih program, membayar lewat metode yang diaktifkan di Admin > Donasi > Pembayaran (kolom kosong =
metode tidak tampil), lalu menekan **Saya sudah bayar**. Konfirmasi tercatat berstatus *menunggu verifikasi*.
Pengurus membuka Admin > Donasi > Donatur, mencocokkan mutasi, mengisi **Nominal**, lalu menekan **Verifikasi**;
saat itu total program bertambah dan angka di situs ikut berubah otomatis. Mengabari admin lewat WhatsApp bersifat opsional.
Ada batas 200 konfirmasi/jam (seluruh situs) dan 20/jam per alamat IP.

import FieldGrid from './FieldGrid.vue'
import DonutChart from './DonutChart.vue'
import { db, supabase, loadDB, note, rp, dt, today, fetchTimes, upsert, removeRow, saveSetting, uploadImage } from './db'
import { CFG, NAV, TABS, normalize } from './schema'

const ready = ref(false)
const authed = ref(false)
const email = ref('')
const view = ref('dashboard')
const tab = ref(0)
const side = ref(false)
const busy = ref(false)
const msg = ref({ t: '', bad: false, show: false })
const login = ref({ u: '', p: '', err: '' })
const draft = ref({})
const dlg = ref(null) // { i, model }
const pass = ref({ cur: '', np: '', rp: '' })
const acct = ref({ name: '', avatar: null, newEmail: '', curE: '', since: '', last: '' })
const showPass = ref(false)
const loginBusy = ref(false)
const year = new Date().getFullYear()

const ICON = { dashboard: Squares2X2Icon, profil: BuildingLibraryIcon, sholat: ClockIcon, donasi: HeartIcon,
  kegiatan: UserGroupIcon, artikel: NewspaperIcon, kontak: PhoneIcon, akun: Cog6ToothIcon }
const STAT_ICON = [DocumentTextIcon, UserGroupIcon, HeartIcon, BanknotesIcon]
const GROUPS = [['Utama', ['dashboard']], ['Konten situs', ['profil', 'kegiatan', 'artikel', 'kontak']], ['Donasi & ibadah', ['donasi', 'sholat']], ['Sistem', ['akun']]]
const LABEL = Object.fromEntries(NAV)
const SUB = {
  dashboard: 'Ringkasan aktivitas masjid dan donasi.', profil: 'Informasi dasar dan galeri foto masjid.',
  sholat: 'Lokasi dan waktu sholat yang tampil di beranda.', donasi: 'Program, metode pembayaran, dan riwayat donatur. Konfirmasi WhatsApp memakai kontak pertama di menu Kontak.',
  kegiatan: 'Program, jadwal kajian, dan foto untuk halaman Program.',
  artikel: 'Berita dan artikel untuk jamaah.', kontak: 'Kontak dan media sosial pengurus.', akun: 'Keamanan akun admin.',
}
let timer

const newCat = ref('')
const saveCats = async (list) => { db.kategori = list; await saveSetting('kategori') }
async function addCat() {
  const n = newCat.value.trim()
  if (!n) return
  if (db.kategori.some((c) => c.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase())) return toast('Kategori sudah ada.', true)
  try { await saveCats([...db.kategori, n]); newCat.value = ''; toast('Kategori ditambahkan.') } catch (e) { fail(e) }
}
async function renameCat(old) {
  const n = (prompt('Nama baru kategori:', old) || '').trim()
  if (!n || n === old) return
  if (db.kategori.some((c) => c !== old && c.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase())) return toast('Kategori sudah ada.', true)
  try {
    await saveCats(db.kategori.map((c) => (c === old ? n : c)))
    for (const a of db.ARTICLES.filter((x) => x.category === old)) { a.category = n; await upsert('ARTICLES', a) }
    toast('Kategori diubah, artikelnya ikut diperbarui.')
  } catch (e) { await loadDB(); fail(e) }
}
async function delCat(c) {
  const used = db.ARTICLES.filter((a) => a.category === c).length
  if (used) return toast(`Dipakai ${used} artikel. Ubah kategori artikelnya dulu.`, true)
  if (!confirm(`Hapus kategori "${c}"?`)) return
  try { await saveCats(db.kategori.filter((x) => x !== c)); toast('Kategori dihapus.') } catch (e) { fail(e) }
}

const latest = computed(() => [...db.ARTICLES].sort((a, b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date))).slice(0, 4))

const cfg = computed(() => CFG[view.value === 'donasi' ? TABS[tab.value][0] : view.value])
const rows = computed(() => (cfg.value?.key ? db[cfg.value.key] : []))
const clone = (o) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o ?? {}))

function toast(t, bad = false) {
  msg.value = { t, bad, show: true }
  clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => (msg.value.show = false), 3000)
}
const fail = (e) => toast('Gagal: ' + (e?.message || 'periksa koneksi'), true)
function go(v) { view.value = v; side.value = false }
watch([view, tab], () => { if (cfg.value?.single) draft.value = clone(db[cfg.value.single]) })

let channel
function listen() {
  if (channel || !supabase) return
  channel = supabase.channel('admin-donors')
    .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'donors' }, (p) => {
      const row = p.new.data
      if (db.DONORS.some((d) => d.id === row.id)) return
      db.DONORS.push(row); toast(`Donasi baru masuk dari ${row.name}, menunggu verifikasi.`)
    }).subscribe()
}
onBeforeUnmount(() => channel && supabase.removeChannel(channel))

async function boot() {
  try {
    await loadDB(); listen()
    const h = db.sholat
    if (h.auto && h.lat && h.lng && h.updated !== today()) {
      try { Object.assign(h, await fetchTimes(h), { updated: today() }); await saveSetting('sholat') } catch { /* offline */ }
    }
  } catch (e) { fail(e) }
}
async function doLogin() {
  login.value.err = ''; loginBusy.value = true
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: login.value.u.trim(), password: login.value.p })
    if (error) {
      const m = (error.message || '').toLowerCase()
      login.value.err = m.includes('not confirmed') ? 'Email belum dikonfirmasi. Buka Supabase > Authentication > Users dan konfirmasi akun ini.'
        : error.status === 429 || m.includes('rate limit') ? 'Terlalu banyak percobaan. Coba lagi beberapa menit lagi.'
        : error.status === 0 || m.includes('fetch') ? 'Tidak dapat terhubung ke server. Periksa koneksi internet.'
        : m.includes('invalid login credentials') ? 'Email atau password salah.'
        : 'Gagal masuk: ' + (error.message || 'kesalahan tidak diketahui') + '. Periksa VITE_SUPABASE_URL dan VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY di file .env.'
      return
    }
    applyUser(data.user); authed.value = true; await boot()
  } catch { login.value.err = 'Tidak dapat terhubung ke server. Periksa koneksi internet.' } finally { loginBusy.value = false }
}
async function logout() { if (channel) { supabase.removeChannel(channel); channel = null } await supabase.auth.signOut(); authed.value = false; login.value = { u: '', p: '', err: '' } }

async function saveSingle() {
  const c = cfg.value, n = normalize(c.f, draft.value)
  if (c.single === 'sholat') n.updated = today()
  busy.value = true
  try { db[c.single] = n; await saveSetting(c.single); note(c.t + ' diperbarui'); toast('Tersimpan.') } catch (e) { fail(e) } finally { busy.value = false }
}
async function autoTimes() {
  const d = draft.value
  if (!d.lat || !d.lng) return toast('Isi latitude dan longitude dulu.', true)
  try { Object.assign(d, await fetchTimes(d)); toast('Jadwal terisi. Klik Simpan untuk menerapkan.') }
  catch { toast('Gagal mengambil jadwal. Cek koneksi atau isi manual.', true) }
}
function openForm(i) {
  const c = cfg.value
  dlg.value = { i, model: clone(i == null ? (c.def ? c.def() : {}) : rows.value[i]) }
}
async function submitForm() {
  const c = cfg.value, { i, model } = dlg.value
  const o = normalize(c.f, model); c.pre?.(o, model)
  if (c.key === 'DONORS' && !o.program) return toast('Buat program donasi dulu.', true)
  if (c.key === 'METODE' && o.tipe === 'qris' && !o.gambar) return toast('Unggah gambar QRIS dulu.', true)
  if (c.key === 'DONORS' && o.status === 'verified' && !(o.amount > 0)) return toast('Isi nominal dulu sebelum memverifikasi.', true)
  const prev = i == null ? null : { ...rows.value[i] }
  busy.value = true
  try {
    c.hook?.(prev, o) // donasi terverifikasi memperbarui total program
    await upsert(c.key, o)
    if (c.key === 'DONORS') await Promise.all(db.PROGRAMS.map((p) => upsert('PROGRAMS', p)))
    if (i == null) rows.value.push(o); else rows.value[i] = o
    note((i == null ? 'Menambah ' : 'Mengubah ') + c.item + ': ' + (o.title || o.name))
    dlg.value = null; toast('Tersimpan.')
  } catch (e) { await loadDB(); fail(e) } finally { busy.value = false }
}
async function remove(i) {
  const c = cfg.value, old = rows.value[i]
  if (!confirm(`Hapus ${c.item} ini? Tindakan tidak bisa dibatalkan.`)) return
  try {
    c.hook?.(old, null)
    await removeRow(c.key, old.id)
    if (c.key === 'DONORS') await Promise.all(db.PROGRAMS.map((p) => upsert('PROGRAMS', p)))
    rows.value.splice(i, 1)
    note('Menghapus ' + c.item + ': ' + (old.title || old.name)); toast('Terhapus.')
  } catch (e) { await loadDB(); fail(e) }
}
async function verify(i) {
  const c = cfg.value, prev = { ...rows.value[i] }, o = { ...prev, status: 'verified' }
  if (!(prev.amount > 0)) { openForm(i); return toast('Isi nominal sesuai mutasi dulu, lalu ubah status ke Terverifikasi.', true) }
  try {
    c.hook(prev, o)
    await upsert('DONORS', o)
    await Promise.all(db.PROGRAMS.map((p) => upsert('PROGRAMS', p)))
    rows.value[i] = o; note('Verifikasi donasi: ' + o.name); toast('Donasi diverifikasi, total program bertambah.')
  } catch (e) { await loadDB(); fail(e) }
}
function quickAdd(v, t = 0) { view.value = v; tab.value = t; openForm(null) }

function applyUser(u) {
  email.value = u.email
  const m = u.user_metadata || {}
  Object.assign(acct.value, { name: m.name || '', avatar: m.avatar_url ? { src: m.avatar_url } : null, since: u.created_at || '', last: u.last_sign_in_at || '' })
}
const when = (iso) => (iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString('id-ID', { dateStyle: 'long', timeStyle: 'short' }) : '—')
async function verifyCurrent(pw) {
  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.value, password: pw })
  if (error) toast('Password saat ini salah.', true)
  return !error
}
async function pickAvatar(e) {
  const file = e.target.files[0]
  if (!file) return
  try { acct.value.avatar = await uploadImage(file); toast('Foto dipilih. Klik Simpan profil.') } catch (err) { fail(err) } finally { e.target.value = '' }
}
async function saveProfile() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ data: { name: acct.value.name.trim(), avatar_url: acct.value.avatar?.src || null } })
  if (error) return fail(error)
  applyUser(data.user); toast('Profil diperbarui.')
}
async function saveEmail() {
  const ne = acct.value.newEmail.trim()
  if (!ne || ne === email.value) return toast('Isi email baru yang berbeda.', true)
  if (!(await verifyCurrent(acct.value.curE))) return
  const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ email: ne })
  if (error) return fail(error)
  acct.value.curE = ''; acct.value.newEmail = ''
  toast('Tautan konfirmasi dikirim ke email. Email baru aktif setelah dikonfirmasi.')
}
async function savePassword() {
  const p = pass.value
  if (p.np.length < 8 || p.np !== p.rp) return toast('Password baru minimal 8 karakter dan harus sama.', true)
  if (!(await verifyCurrent(p.cur))) return
  const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: p.np })
  if (error) return fail(error)
  pass.value = { cur: '', np: '', rp: '' }; toast('Password diperbarui.')
}
async function signOutAll() {
  if (!confirm('Keluar dari semua perangkat, termasuk perangkat ini?')) return
  if (channel) { supabase.removeChannel(channel); channel = null }
  await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: 'global' }); authed.value = false; login.value = { u: '', p: '', err: '' }
}

const pageTitle = computed(() => LABEL[view.value] || '')
const initial = computed(() => ((acct.value.name || email.value)[0] || 'A').toUpperCase())
const pendingCount = computed(() => db.DONORS.filter((d) => d.status === 'pending').length)
const todayLabel = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })
const stats = computed(() => [
  [db.ARTICLES.filter((a) => a.status !== 'draft').length, 'Total artikel terbit'],
  [db.ACTIVITIES.length, 'Total program masjid'],
  [db.PROGRAMS.filter((p) => p.status !== 'selesai').length, 'Program donasi aktif'],
  [rp(db.DONORS.filter((d) => d.status !== 'pending').reduce((s, d) => s + (d.amount || 0), 0)), 'Total donasi tercatat'],
])
const perProgram = computed(() => db.PROGRAMS.map((p) => [p.name, db.DONORS.filter((d) => d.program === p.id && d.status !== 'pending').reduce((s, d) => s + (d.amount || 0), 0)]))

onMounted(async () => {
  supabase?.auth.onAuthStateChange((ev) => { if (ev === 'SIGNED_OUT') authed.value = false })
  if (supabase) {
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
    if (data.session) { applyUser(data.session.user); authed.value = true; await boot() }
  }
  ready.value = true
})
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="ready" class="adm">
    <!-- ===== Login ===== -->
    <section v-if="!authed" class="a-login">
      <form class="a-login-card" @submit.prevent="doLogin">
        <div class="a-login-head">
          <BrandMark />
          <div><strong>Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah</strong><small>Panel Pengurus</small></div>
        </div>
        <h1 class="sr-only">Masuk admin</h1>

        <p v-if="!supabase" class="a-alert err" role="alert"><ExclamationTriangleIcon class="size-5" /><span>Supabase belum dikonfigurasi. Isi <b>VITE_SUPABASE_URL</b> dan <b>VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY</b> di file .env, lalu jalankan ulang.</span></p>
        <p v-if="login.err" class="a-alert err" role="alert"><ExclamationTriangleIcon class="size-5" /><span>{{ login.err }}</span></p>

        <div class="fgrid" style="grid-template-columns:1fr">
          <div class="fld">
            <label for="lu">Email</label>
            <div class="a-inp"><EnvelopeIcon /><input id="lu" v-model="login.u" type="email" autocomplete="username" placeholder="nama@email.com" required></div>
          </div>
          <div class="fld">
            <label for="lp">Password</label>
            <div class="a-inp">
              <LockClosedIcon />
              <input id="lp" v-model="login.p" :type="showPass ? 'text' : 'password'" autocomplete="current-password" placeholder="Masukkan password" required>
              <button type="button" class="a-eye" :aria-label="showPass ? 'Sembunyikan password' : 'Tampilkan password'" @click="showPass = !showPass">
                <EyeSlashIcon v-if="showPass" class="size-5" /><EyeIcon v-else class="size-5" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="a-btn a-btn-p a-btn-block" style="margin-top:24px" type="submit" :disabled="loginBusy || !supabase">{{ loginBusy ? 'Memproses…' : 'Masuk' }}</button>
        <a class="a-back" href="#beranda">← Kembali ke website</a>
      </form>
      <small class="a-login-foot">© {{ year }} Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah</small>
    </section>

    <!-- ===== Dashboard admin ===== -->
    <div v-else class="a-app">
      <div v-if="side" class="a-scrim" @click="side = false"></div>
      <aside class="a-side" :class="{ open: side }">
        <div class="a-brandrow"><BrandMark /><div><strong>Masjid Al-Barokah</strong><small>Panel Pengurus</small></div></div>
        <nav class="a-nav" aria-label="Menu admin">
          <template v-for="[label, keys] in GROUPS" :key="label">
            <div class="a-navlabel">{{ label }}</div>
            <button v-for="k in keys" :key="k" type="button" class="a-navbtn" :class="{ on: view === k }" :aria-current="view === k ? 'page' : undefined" @click="go(k)">
              <component :is="ICON[k]" class="size-5" />{{ LABEL[k] }}
            </button>
          </template>
        </nav>
        <div class="a-user">
          <div class="a-userrow"><span class="a-avatar" aria-hidden="true"><img v-if="acct.avatar" :src="acct.avatar.src" alt=""><span v-else>{{ initial }}</span></span><div><b>{{ acct.name || 'Admin' }}</b><span>{{ email }}</span></div></div>
          <button type="button" class="a-logout" @click="logout"><ArrowRightOnRectangleIcon class="size-4" /> Keluar</button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div>
        <header class="a-top">
          <button class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-menubtn" aria-label="Buka menu" @click="side = !side"><Bars3Icon class="size-5" /></button>
          <span class="a-crumb">Admin / <b>{{ pageTitle }}</b></span>
          <a class="a-btn a-btn-sm" style="margin-left:auto" href="#beranda" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Lihat website <ArrowTopRightOnSquareIcon class="size-4" /></a>
        </header>

        <main id="main" class="a-main" tabindex="-1">
          <!-- Dashboard -->
          <template v-if="view === 'dashboard'">
            <div class="a-pagehead"><div><h2>Assalamu'alaikum</h2><p>{{ todayLabel }} · {{ SUB.dashboard }}</p></div></div>
            <p v-if="pendingCount" class="a-alert warn" role="status"><ExclamationTriangleIcon class="size-5" /><span><b>{{ pendingCount }} donasi menunggu verifikasi.</b> <a href="#admin" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline" @click.prevent="view = 'donasi'; tab = 2">Tinjau sekarang</a></span></p>

            <div class="a-stats">
              <div v-for="([n, l], i) in stats" :key="l" class="a-card a-stat"><span class="a-statico"><component :is="STAT_ICON[i]" class="size-6" /></span><div><b>{{ n }}</b><span>{{ l }}</span></div></div>
            </div>
            <div class="a-grid">
              <div class="a-card"><h3>Artikel terbaru</h3>
                <ul v-if="latest.length" class="a-list"><li v-for="a in latest" :key="a.id"><b>{{ a.title }}</b><br><span class="muted">{{ dt(a.date) }} · {{ a.category }}</span></li></ul>
                <p v-else class="muted">Belum ada artikel.</p></div>
              <div class="a-card"><h3>Donasi terverifikasi per program</h3>
                <DonutChart :key="perProgram.length + rp(stats[3][0])" :labels="perProgram.map((x) => x[0])" :data="perProgram.map((x) => x[1])" /></div>
              <div class="a-card" style="grid-column:1/-1"><h3>Aktivitas terbaru</h3>
                <ul v-if="db.log.length" class="a-list"><li v-for="l in db.log" :key="l.at">{{ l.t }} <span class="muted">· {{ new Date(l.at).toLocaleString('id-ID', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }) }}</span></li></ul>
                <p v-else class="muted">Belum ada aktivitas.</p></div>
            </div>
            <div class="a-quick">
              <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" @click="quickAdd('artikel')"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah artikel</button>
              <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" @click="quickAdd('kegiatan')"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah kegiatan</button>
              <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" @click="quickAdd('donasi', 0)"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah program donasi</button>
            </div>
          </template>

          <!-- Akun -->
          <template v-else-if="view === 'akun'">
            <div class="a-pagehead"><div><h2>Pengaturan Akun</h2><p>{{ SUB.akun }}</p></div></div>
            <div class="a-stack">
              <form class="a-card" @submit.prevent="saveProfile">
                <h3><UserCircleIcon class="size-5" /> Profil</h3>
                <div class="a-profile">
                  <span class="a-avatar a-avatar-lg" aria-hidden="true"><img v-if="acct.avatar" :src="acct.avatar.src" alt=""><span v-else>{{ initial }}</span></span>
                  <div class="fld">
                    <label for="avf">Foto profil</label>
                    <input id="avf" type="file" accept="image/*" @change="pickAvatar">
                    <button v-if="acct.avatar" type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-btn-d" style="justify-self:start" @click="acct.avatar = null">Hapus foto</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="fgrid">
                  <div class="fld"><label for="an">Nama tampilan</label><input id="an" v-model="acct.name" maxlength="60" placeholder="mis. Pengurus DKM"></div>
                  <div class="fld"><label for="ae0">Email saat ini</label><input id="ae0" :value="email" disabled></div>
                </div>
                <div class="a-foot"><button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit">Simpan profil</button></div>
              </form>

              <form class="a-card" @submit.prevent="saveEmail">
                <h3><AtSymbolIcon class="size-5" /> Ganti email</h3>
                <div class="fgrid">
                  <div class="fld"><label for="ae1">Email baru</label><input id="ae1" v-model="acct.newEmail" type="email" required autocomplete="email"></div>
                  <div class="fld"><label for="ae2">Password saat ini</label><input id="ae2" v-model="acct.curE" type="password" required autocomplete="current-password"></div>
                </div>
                <p class="muted" style="margin:12px 0 0;font-size:13px">Supabase mengirim tautan konfirmasi ke email. Email baru baru aktif setelah tautan diklik, jadi pastikan alamatnya benar.</p>
                <div class="a-foot"><button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit">Ganti email</button></div>
              </form>

              <form class="a-card" @submit.prevent="savePassword">
                <h3><KeyIcon class="size-5" /> Ganti password</h3>
                <div class="fgrid">
                  <div class="fld full"><label for="ap0">Password saat ini</label><input id="ap0" v-model="pass.cur" type="password" required autocomplete="current-password"></div>
                  <div class="fld"><label for="ap">Password baru</label><input id="ap" v-model="pass.np" type="password" minlength="8" required autocomplete="new-password"><small>Minimal 8 karakter.</small></div>
                  <div class="fld"><label for="ar">Ulangi password baru</label><input id="ar" v-model="pass.rp" type="password" required autocomplete="new-password"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="a-foot"><button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit">Simpan password</button></div>
              </form>

              <div class="a-card">
                <h3><ShieldCheckIcon class="size-5" /> Keamanan &amp; sesi</h3>
                <dl class="a-info">
                  <dt>Akun dibuat</dt><dd>{{ when(acct.since) }}</dd>
                  <dt>Login terakhir</dt><dd>{{ when(acct.last) }}</dd>
                </dl>
                <p class="muted" style="margin:14px 0 0;font-size:13px">Jika Anda login di perangkat lain atau curiga akun diakses orang lain, keluarkan semua sesi lalu ganti password.</p>
                <div class="a-foot"><button type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-d" @click="signOutAll"><ArrowRightOnRectangleIcon class="size-4" /> Keluar dari semua perangkat</button></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </template>

          <!-- Modul data (daftar / form tunggal) -->
          <template v-else>
            <div class="a-pagehead">
              <div><h2>{{ pageTitle }}</h2><p>{{ SUB[view] }}</p></div>
              <button v-if="!cfg.single" class="a-btn a-btn-p" @click="openForm(null)"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah {{ cfg.item }}</button>
            </div>

            <TabGroup v-if="view === 'donasi'" :selected-index="tab" @change="tab = $event">
              <TabList class="a-tabs"><Tab v-for="[k, l] in TABS" :key="k" v-slot="{ selected }" as="template"><button type="button" :class="{ on: selected }">{{ l }}</button></Tab></TabList>
            </TabGroup>
            
            <div v-if="view === 'artikel'" class="a-card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <h3>Kategori artikel</h3>
              <ul class="flex flex-wrap gap-2" style="list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0 0 14px">
                <li v-for="c in db.kategori" :key="c" class="a-badge" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
                  {{ c }}
                  <button type="button" aria-label="Ubah kategori" @click="renameCat(c)"><PencilSquareIcon class="size-4" /></button>
                  <button type="button" aria-label="Hapus kategori" @click="delCat(c)"><TrashIcon class="size-4" /></button>
                </li>
              </ul>
              <form class="flex gap-2" @submit.prevent="addCat">
                <input v-model="newCat" maxlength="30" placeholder="Kategori baru" aria-label="Kategori baru">
                <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah</button>
              </form>
            </div>

            <template v-if="cfg.single">
              <form :key="cfg.single" class="a-card" @submit.prevent="saveSingle">
                <h3>{{ cfg.t }}</h3>
                <FieldGrid :fields="cfg.f" :model="draft" />
                <p v-if="cfg.note" class="muted" style="margin:12px 0 0;font-size:13px">{{ cfg.note }}</p>
                <div class="a-foot">
                  <button v-if="cfg.tools" type="button" class="a-btn" @click="autoTimes">Ambil jadwal otomatis</button>
                  <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit" :disabled="busy">{{ busy ? 'Menyimpan…' : 'Simpan perubahan' }}</button>
                </div>
              </form>
            </template>

            <div v-else class="a-card a-tw">
              <table v-if="rows.length">
                <thead><tr><th v-for="c in cfg.cols" :key="c[0]">{{ c[0] }}</th><th></th></tr></thead>
                <tbody><tr v-for="(r, i) in rows" :key="r.id || i">
                  <td v-for="c in cfg.cols" :key="c[0]">
                    <span v-if="c[2] === 'badge'" class="a-badge" :class="{ w: c[1](r)[1] }">{{ c[1](r)[0] }}</span>
                    <template v-else>{{ c[1](r) }}</template>
                  </td>
                  <td class="a-act">
                    <button v-if="cfg.key === 'DONORS' && r.status === 'pending'" class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-btn-p" @click="verify(i)">Verifikasi</button>
                    <button class="a-btn a-btn-sm" aria-label="Ubah" @click="openForm(i)"><PencilSquareIcon class="size-4" /></button>
                    <button class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-btn-d" aria-label="Hapus" @click="remove(i)"><TrashIcon class="size-4" /></button>
                  </td>
                </tr></tbody>
              </table>
              <div v-else class="a-empty">
                <InboxIcon class="size-10" /><b style="color:var(--ink)">Belum ada data</b><span>Mulai dengan menambahkan {{ cfg.item }} pertama.</span>
                <button class="a-btn a-btn-p" @click="openForm(null)"><PlusIcon class="size-4" /> Tambah {{ cfg.item }}</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </template>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="a-toast" :class="{ show: msg.show, bad: msg.bad }" role="status">{{ msg.t }}</div>
  </div>

  <Dialog :open="!!dlg" class="adm relative z-50" @close="dlg = null">
    <div class="fixed inset-0 bg-ink/60" aria-hidden="true" />
    <div class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto p-3 grid place-items-start sm:place-items-center">
      <DialogPanel v-if="dlg" class="a-card a-dlg w-full max-w-3xl">
        <form @submit.prevent="submitForm">
          <h3>{{ dlg.i == null ? 'Tambah' : 'Ubah' }} {{ cfg.item }}</h3>
          <FieldGrid :fields="cfg.f" :model="dlg.model" />
          <div class="a-foot"><button type="button" class="a-btn" @click="dlg = null">Batal</button><button class="a-btn a-btn-p" type="submit" :disabled="busy">{{ busy ? 'Menyimpan…' : 'Simpan' }}</button></div>
        </form>
      </DialogPanel>
    </div>
  </Dialog>
</template>


=== src/admin/db.js ===
import { reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import * as C from '../data/content'

// Hanya alamat dasarnya yang dipakai (mis. https://xxxx.supabase.co), walau .env terisi dengan tambahan /rest/v1/
let SB_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL
try { SB_URL = new URL(SB_URL).origin } catch { /* biarkan apa adanya */ }
const KEY = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
/** null bila .env belum diisi: situs publik tetap jalan dengan data bawaan. */
export const supabase = SB_URL && KEY ? createClient(SB_URL, KEY) : null

const TABLE = { PROGRAMS: 'programs', DONORS: 'donors', METODE: 'metode', ACTIVITIES: 'activities', ARTICLES: 'articles' }

export const rp = (n) => 'Rp' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(n || 0)
export const today = () => new Date().toLocaleDateString('sv', { timeZone: 'Asia/Jakarta' })
export const dt = (iso) => (iso ? new Date(iso + 'T00:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' }) : '—')
export const esc = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]))
export const slug = (s) => String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 40) + '-' + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4)
export const fmtBody = (t) => (/<\w+[^>]*>/.test(t) ? t : t.split(/\n{2,}/).filter(Boolean).map((b) => (b.startsWith('## ') ? `<h2>${esc(b.slice(3))}</h2>` : `<p>${esc(b).replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}</p>`)).join(''))

const must = ({ data, error }) => { if (error) throw error; return data }
const put = (arr, items) => arr.splice(0, arr.length, ...items)

/** Unggah gambar ke Supabase Storage (bucket "media"), dikecilkan dulu agar hemat kuota. */
export async function uploadImage(file, name = '') {
  if (!supabase) throw new Error('Supabase belum dikonfigurasi')
  if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) throw new Error('File harus berupa gambar')
  const bmp = await createImageBitmap(file)
  const r = Math.min(1, 1600 / Math.max(bmp.width, bmp.height))
  const c = document.createElement('canvas')
  c.width = Math.round(bmp.width * r); c.height = Math.round(bmp.height * r)
  c.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, c.width, c.height)
  const blob = await new Promise((ok, no) => c.toBlob((b) => (b ? ok(b) : no(new Error('Gagal memproses gambar'))), 'image/jpeg', 0.82))
  const base = String(name || file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '')).toLowerCase().normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 40) || 'gambar'
  const path = `${new Date().getFullYear()}/${base}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}.jpg`
  const { error } = await supabase.storage.from('media').upload(path, blob, { contentType: 'image/jpeg', cacheControl: '31536000' })
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(/bucket not found/i.test(error.message)
      ? 'Bucket "media" belum ada di Supabase. Jalankan supabase/media.sql di SQL Editor.'
      : error.message)
  }
  return { src: supabase.storage.from('media').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl, width: c.width, height: c.height, alt: '' }
}

export async function fetchTimes(h) {
  const d = today().split('-').reverse().join('-') // DD-MM-YYYY, tanggal WIB
  const r = await fetch(`https://api.aladhan.com/v1/timings/${d}?latitude=${h.lat}&longitude=${h.lng}&method=${h.metode || 20}&timezonestring=Asia/Jakarta`)
  const t = (await r.json()).data.timings, c = (x) => x.slice(0, 5)
  return { subuh: c(t.Fajr), dzuhur: c(t.Dhuhr), ashar: c(t.Asr), maghrib: c(t.Maghrib), isya: c(t.Isha) }
}

/* ---------------- Situs publik: baca data dari Supabase ---------------- */
/** Naik setiap kali data donasi berubah, supaya halaman yang memakainya menghitung ulang. */
export const live = ref(0)
const getRows = async (table) => must(await supabase.from(table).select('data').order('pos')).map((r) => r.data)

/** Daftar donatur publik lewat fungsi database; jatuh ke view lama bila fungsinya belum dibuat. */
async function getDonors() {
  const r = await supabase.rpc('public_donor_list')
  if (!r.error) return r.data || []
  return getRows('public_donors')
}

/** Program + donatur: dipakai saat start dan setiap ada perubahan realtime. */
async function loadDonations() {
  const [programs, donors] = await Promise.all([getRows('programs'), getDonors()])
  if (programs.length) put(C.PROGRAMS, programs.filter((p) => p.status !== 'selesai'))
  put(C.DONORS, donors)
  put(C.TRANSACTIONS, donors.slice().sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date)).slice(0, 10)
    .map((d) => ({ date: d.date, program: d.program, amount: d.amount, status: 'Terverifikasi' })))
}

/** Situs publik ikut berubah saat pengurus memverifikasi donasi (tanpa refresh). */
export function watchPublic() {
  if (!supabase) return
  let t
  supabase.channel('public-live')
    .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'programs' }, () => {
      clearTimeout(t)
      t = setTimeout(() => loadDonations().then(() => live.value++).catch(() => {}), 600)
    })
    .subscribe()
}

/** Catat konfirmasi donasi lewat fungsi database. Selalu berstatus menunggu; pengurus yang memverifikasi. */
export async function submitDonation(row) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('submit_donation', { p: row })
  if (error) throw error
  return data
}

/** Muat ulang angka donasi sekarang juga (tanpa menunggu realtime). */
export async function refreshDonations() { await loadDonations(); live.value++ }

export async function syncPublic() {
  if (!supabase) return
  try {
    const [activities, articles, metode, settings] = await Promise.all([
      getRows('activities'), getRows('articles'), getRows('metode').catch(() => []),
      supabase.from('settings').select('key,data').then(must),
      loadDonations(),
    ])
    put(C.PAYMENT.metode, metode.filter((m) => m.aktif !== false))
if (activities.length) put(C.ACTIVITIES, activities)
if (articles.length) put(C.ARTICLES, articles.filter((a) => a.status !== 'draft'))

    const s = Object.fromEntries(settings.map((r) => [r.key, r.data]))
    const pr = s.profil
    if (pr) {
      for (const k of ['alamat', 'tahun', 'kapasitas', 'lantai', 'visi']) {
        if (pr[k] !== '' && pr[k] != null) C.PROFILE[k] = pr[k]
      }
      if (pr.misi?.length) C.PROFILE.misi = pr.misi
    }
    const galeri = s.profil?.galeri
    if (galeri?.length) put(C.GALLERY, galeri.map((g) => ({ src: g.src, alt: g.alt || 'Foto masjid', caption: g.caption || g.alt || '' })))
    const h = s.sholat || {}
    const apply = () => C.PRAYERS.forEach((p) => { const v = h[p.name.toLowerCase()]; if (v) p.time = v })
    apply()
    if (h.auto && h.lat && h.lng && h.updated !== today()) fetchTimes(h).then((t) => { Object.assign(h, t); apply() }).catch(() => {})

    const k = s.kontak
    if (k) {
      if (k.maps) C.PROFILE.maps = k.maps
      const num = (v) => (v === '' || v == null || !Number.isFinite(Number(v)) ? null : Number(v))
      if (num(k.lat) !== null && num(k.lng) !== null) { C.PROFILE.lat = num(k.lat); C.PROFILE.lng = num(k.lng); C.PROFILE.hasCoords = true }
      const wa = (k.wa_list || []).map((l) => {
        const parts = String(l).split('|'), num = parts.pop()
        let n = num.replace(/\D/g, ''); if (n.startsWith('0')) n = '62' + n.slice(1)
        return n ? { label: parts.join('|').trim() || 'WhatsApp', n } : null
      }).filter(Boolean)
      if (!wa.length && k.wa) wa.push({ label: 'WhatsApp', n: k.wa })
      if (wa.length) C.PAYMENT.wa = wa[0].n // nomor konfirmasi donasi = kontak WhatsApp pertama
      const link = (v) => { v = String(v || '').trim(); return v && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(v) ? 'https://' + v : v }
      const shown = (u) => u.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?/i, '').replace(/\/$/, '')
      const ig = String(k.instagram || '').trim().replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?instagram\.com\//i, '').replace(/^@/, '').replace(/[/?].*$/, '')
      const fb = link(k.facebook), yt = link(k.youtube)
      const items = wa.map((w) => ({ label: w.label, value: '+' + w.n, href: 'https://wa.me/' + w.n, icon: 'whatsapp' }))
      if (ig) items.push({ label: 'Instagram', value: '@' + ig, href: 'https://www.instagram.com/' + ig, icon: 'instagram' })
      if (fb) items.push({ label: 'Facebook', value: shown(fb), href: fb, icon: 'facebook' })
      if (yt) items.push({ label: 'YouTube', value: shown(yt), href: yt, icon: 'youtube' })
      if (k.email) items.push({ label: 'Email', value: k.email, href: 'mailto:' + k.email, icon: 'mail', hint: 'Surat resmi & kerja sama' })
      if (items.length) {
        items.push({ label: 'Alamat masjid', value: C.PROFILE.alamat, href: C.PROFILE.maps || '#', icon: 'pin', hint: 'Buka peta lokasi' })
        put(C.CONTACT_ITEMS, items)
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Supabase tidak terjangkau, memakai data bawaan:', e.message)
  }
}

/* ---------------- Admin: baca & tulis (perlu login) ---------------- */
export const db = reactive({})

export async function loadDB() {
  const alamat = 'Jl. Ampera Poncol, Babakan, Kec. Setu, Kota Tangerang Selatan, Banten 15315'
  const next = {
    PROGRAMS: [], DONORS: [], METODE: [], ACTIVITIES: [], ARTICLES: [], log: [],
    profil: { nama: "Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah", alamat, tahun: 2021, luas: '500 m²', kapasitas: 180, lantai: 3, fasilitas: [], visi: '', misi: [], galeri: [] },
    sholat: { lokasi: 'Tangerang Selatan', lat: -6.33, lng: 106.69, metode: '20', auto: true },
    kontak: { wa_list: [], wa: '6281298850904', email: '', alamat, maps: '', lat: '', lng: '', instagram: '', facebook: '', youtube: '' },
    kategori: ['Akhlak', 'Ibadah', 'Berita', 'Hadis', 'Kajian'],
  }
  for (const [key, table] of Object.entries(TABLE)) {
    next[key] = must(await supabase.from(table).select('data').order('pos')).map((r) => r.data)
  }
  for (const r of must(await supabase.from('settings').select('key,data'))) {
    next[r.key] = Array.isArray(next[r.key]) ? r.data : { ...next[r.key], ...r.data }
  }
    for (const a of next.ARTICLES) if (a.category && !next.kategori.includes(a.category)) next.kategori.push(a.category)
  next.PROGRAMS.forEach((p) => { p.type ||= p.target ? 'target' : 'umum'; p.status ||= 'aktif' })
  Object.assign(db, next)
}

export const upsert = async (key, row) => must(await supabase.from(TABLE[key]).upsert({ id: row.id, data: row }))
export const removeRow = async (key, id) => must(await supabase.from(TABLE[key]).delete().eq('id', id))
export const saveSetting = async (key) => must(await supabase.from('settings').upsert({ key, data: db[key] }))

export function note(t) {
  db.log.unshift({ t, at: Date.now() }); db.log = db.log.slice(0, 8)
  saveSetting('log').catch(() => {})
}

=== src/admin/FieldGrid.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { uploadImage } from './db'
const props = defineProps({ fields: Array, model: Object })
const busy = ref('')
const guess = (n) => {
  const s = n.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ').trim()
  return /^(img|dsc|pxl|photo|whatsapp|screenshot)/i.test(s) || !/[a-z]{3,}/i.test(s) ? '' : s
}
async function pick(e, f) {
  const file = e.target.files[0]
  if (!file) return
  busy.value = f.k
  try {
    const hint = guess(file.name)
    const img = await uploadImage(file, hint)
    if (f.t === 'image') props.model[f.k] = img
    else { img.alt = hint; (props.model[f.k] ||= []).push(img) }
  } catch (err) { alert('Gagal unggah gambar: ' + (err.message || err)) } finally { busy.value = ''; e.target.value = '' }
}
function move(k, i, d) {
  const a = props.model[k], j = i + d
  if (j < 0 || j >= a.length) return
  ;[a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]]
}
const visible = computed(() => props.fields.filter((f) => !f.show || f.show(props.model)))
const options = (f) => (typeof f.o === 'function' ? f.o() : f.o)
const wide = (f) => f.full || ['textarea', 'lines', 'check', 'image', 'images'].includes(f.t)
</script>

<template>
  <div class="fgrid">
    <div v-for="f in visible" :key="f.k" class="fld" :class="{ full: wide(f) }">
      <label v-if="f.t === 'check'"><input v-model="model[f.k]" type="checkbox"> {{ f.l }}</label>
      <template v-else>
        <label :for="'f-' + f.k">{{ f.l }}</label>
        <textarea v-if="f.t === 'textarea'" :id="'f-' + f.k" v-model="model[f.k]" :required="!!f.req" :rows="f.rows || 3"></textarea>
        <textarea v-else-if="f.t === 'lines'" :id="'f-' + f.k" :rows="f.rows || 4" :value="(model[f.k] || []).join('\n')"
                  @input="model[f.k] = $event.target.value.split('\n')"></textarea>
        <div v-else-if="f.t === 'image'">
          <div v-if="model[f.k]" class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
            <img :src="model[f.k].src" alt="" class="h-20 w-32 rounded-lg object-cover">
            <button type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-btn-d" @click="model[f.k] = null">Hapus gambar</button>
          </div>
          <input :id="'f-' + f.k" type="file" accept="image/*" @change="pick($event, f)">
          <small v-if="busy === f.k" class="muted">Mengunggah…</small>
        </div>
        <div v-else-if="f.t === 'images'" class="gimgs">
        <p v-if="!(model[f.k] || []).length" class="muted">Belum ada foto. Unggah foto pertama di bawah.</p>
        <ul class="gimg-list">
          <li v-for="(g, i) in model[f.k] || []" :key="g.src" class="gimg">
            <img :src="g.src" :alt="g.alt || 'Foto tanpa nama'">
            <div class="gimg-body">
              <label :for="`gn-${f.k}-${i}`">Nama foto {{ i + 1 }}</label>
              <input :id="`gn-${f.k}-${i}`" v-model="g.alt" type="text" maxlength="60" required :placeholder="f.ph || 'mis. Tempat wudhu'">
              <small>{{ g.width }} × {{ g.height }} px</small>
            </div>
            <div class="gimg-tools">
              <button type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-sm" aria-label="Geser ke atas" :disabled="i === 0" @click="move(f.k, i, -1)">↑</button>
              <button type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-sm" aria-label="Geser ke bawah" :disabled="i === model[f.k].length - 1" @click="move(f.k, i, 1)">↓</button>
              <button type="button" class="a-btn a-btn-sm a-btn-d" aria-label="Hapus foto" @click="model[f.k].splice(i, 1)">Hapus</button>
            </div>
          </li>
        </ul>
        <input :id="'f-' + f.k" type="file" accept="image/*" @change="pick($event, f)">
        <small v-if="busy === f.k" class="muted">Mengunggah…</small>
      </div>
        <select v-else-if="f.t === 'select'" :id="'f-' + f.k" v-model="model[f.k]">
          <option v-for="[v, l] in options(f)" :key="v" :value="v">{{ l }}</option>
        </select>
        <input v-else :id="'f-' + f.k" v-model="model[f.k]" :type="f.t" :required="!!f.req" :step="f.t === 'number' ? 'any' : undefined">
        <small v-if="f.h" class="muted">{{ f.h }}</small>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>


=== src/admin/schema.js ===
import { db, rp, dt, today, slug, fmtBody } from './db'

const F = (k, l, t = 'text', o = {}) => ({ k, l, t, ...o })
const opts = (a) => a.map((x) => [x, x])
const STATUS = [['aktif', 'Aktif'], ['selesai', 'Selesai']]
const badge = (t, warn) => [t, !!warn]
// Donasi terverifikasi menambah total & jumlah donatur pada programnya
const eff = (d, s) => {
  if (!d || d.status === 'pending') return
  const p = db.PROGRAMS.find((x) => x.id === d.program)
  if (p) { p.collected = Math.max(0, (p.collected || 0) + s * d.amount); p.donors = Math.max(0, (p.donors || 0) + s) }
}

const GROUP_LABEL = { pendidikan: 'Pendidikan', sosial: 'Sosial & pemberdayaan', dakwah: 'Dakwah & kajian' }
const TIPE = { bank: 'Transfer bank', ewallet: 'E-wallet', qris: 'QRIS' }
export const metodeLabel = (m) => (m.tipe === 'bank' ? 'Transfer ' + m.nama : m.nama)

export const CFG = {
  profil: { t: 'Profil Masjid', single: 'profil', f: [

export const NAV = [['dashboard', 'Dashboard'], ['profil', 'Profil Masjid'], ['sholat', 'Jadwal Sholat'], ['donasi', 'Donasi'], ['kegiatan', 'Program Masjid'], ['artikel', 'Artikel / Berita'], ['kontak', 'Kontak'], ['akun', 'Pengaturan Akun']]
export const TABS = [['program', 'Program Donasi'], ['metode', 'Pembayaran'], ['donatur', 'Donatur']]

/** Ubah isi form (string dari input) menjadi tipe data yang benar. */
export function normalize(fields, m) {
  const o = { ...m }
  fields.forEach((f) => {
    const v = m[f.k]
    if (f.t === 'number') o[f.k] = v === '' || v == null ? null : Number(v)
    else if (f.t === 'lines') o[f.k] = (v || []).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    else if (f.t === 'images') o[f.k] = v || []
    else if (f.t === 'check') o[f.k] = !!v
    else o[f.k] = typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : v
  })
  return o
}


=== src/App.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, defineAsyncComponent } from 'vue'
import { Disclosure, DisclosureButton } from '@headlessui/vue'
import { Bars3Icon, XMarkIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import BrandMark from './components/BrandMark.vue'
import HomeView from './views/HomeView.vue'
import ProfilView from './views/ProfilView.vue'
import DonationView from './views/DonationView.vue'
import NewsView from './views/NewsView.vue'
import DonationDialog from './components/DonationDialog.vue'
import { CONTACT_ITEMS } from './data/content'
import { store } from './store'
import ProgramView from './views/ProgramView.vue'

// Donasi tidak ada di NAV: di header cukup satu tombol "Donasi".
const NAV = [
  ['beranda', 'Beranda'], ['profil', 'Profil'], ['program', 'Program'], ['berita', 'Berita'],
]
const FOOTER_NAV = [...NAV, ['donasi', 'Donasi']]
const VIEWS = { beranda: HomeView, profil: ProfilView, donasi: DonationView, program: ProgramView, berita: NewsView }
const AdminApp = defineAsyncComponent(() => import('./admin/AdminApp.vue'))
const year = ref(new Date().getFullYear())

// Footer ikut daftar kontak dari Admin > Kontak: semua WhatsApp, Instagram, email, lalu peta.
const waText = (label) => (/pengurus/i.test(label) ? 'WhatsApp pengurus' : 'WhatsApp ' + label)
const FOOTER_CONTACT = (() => {
  const out = []
  for (const c of CONTACT_ITEMS) if (c.icon === 'whatsapp') out.push({ text: waText(c.label), href: c.href })
  for (const c of CONTACT_ITEMS) {
    if (c.icon === 'instagram') out.push({ text: 'Instagram', href: c.href })
    if (c.icon === 'facebook') out.push({ text: 'Facebook', href: c.href })
    if (c.icon === 'youtube') out.push({ text: 'YouTube', href: c.href })
    if (c.icon === 'mail') out.push({ text: 'Email', href: c.href })
  }
  const pin = CONTACT_ITEMS.find((c) => c.icon === 'pin')
  if (pin) out.push({ text: 'Lihat lokasi di Google Maps', href: pin.href })
  return out
})()
</script>

<template>
  <AdminApp v-if="store.view === 'admin'" />
  <template v-else>
    <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Lewati ke konten utama</a>

    <Disclosure as="header" class="site-header" v-slot="{ open, close }">
      <div class="wrap header-inner">
        <a class="brand" href="#beranda" aria-label="Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah — Beranda" @click.prevent="store.go('beranda')">
          <BrandMark />
          <span class="brand-text">
            <span class="brand-name">Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah</span>
            <span class="brand-sub">Tangerang Selatan</span>
          </span>
        </a>
        <nav class="main-nav" :class="{ 'is-open': open }" aria-label="Navigasi utama">
          <button v-for="[id, label] in NAV" :key="id" type="button" class="nav-link"
                  :aria-current="store.view === id ? 'page' : undefined" @click="store.go(id); close()">{{ label }}</button>
          <!-- hanya tampil di layar kecil (tombol header disembunyikan di sana) -->
          <button type="button" class="nav-link nav-donasi" :aria-current="store.view === 'donasi' ? 'page' : undefined"
                  @click="store.go('donasi'); close()">Donasi</button>
        </nav>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm header-cta" @click="store.go('donasi')">Donasi</button>
          <DisclosureButton class="nav-toggle" :aria-label="open ? 'Tutup menu' : 'Buka menu'">
            <XMarkIcon v-if="open" class="size-6" /><Bars3Icon v-else class="size-6" />
          </DisclosureButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Disclosure>

    <main id="main" tabindex="-1"><component :is="VIEWS[store.view]" /></main>

    <footer class="site-footer">
      <div class="wrap footer-grid">
        <div class="footer-brand">
          <div class="brand brand-footer">
            <BrandMark />
            <span class="brand-text"><span class="brand-name">Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah</span><span class="brand-sub">Tangerang Selatan</span></span>
          </div>
          <p class="footer-desc">Rumah ibadah, majelis ilmu, dan simpul kepedulian warga. Mari bersama merawat masjid dan lingkungan.</p>
        </div>
        <nav class="footer-nav" aria-label="Navigasi footer">
          <span class="footer-heading">Jelajahi</span>
          <button v-for="[id, label] in FOOTER_NAV" :key="id" type="button" @click="store.go(id)">{{ label }}</button>
        </nav>
        <div class="footer-contact">
          <span class="footer-heading">Hubungi</span>
          <a v-for="c in FOOTER_CONTACT" :key="c.text" :href="c.href" target="_blank" rel="noopener">{{ c.text }}</a>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="wrap footer-bottom">
        <p>© {{ year }} Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah.</p>
      </div>
    </footer>

    <DonationDialog />
  </template>
</template>

<script setup>
import { Dialog, DialogPanel, DialogTitle, TransitionRoot, TransitionChild } from '@headlessui/vue'
import { XMarkIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
defineProps({ open: Boolean, title: String, eyebrow: String })
defineEmits(['close'])
// Kelas base-dialog (main.css) menaikkan z-index dialog di atas header situs.
// Jangan taruh komentar HTML di dalam <TransitionRoot>: Headless UI butuh tepat satu elemen anak.
</script>

<template>
  <TransitionRoot :show="open" as="template">
    <Dialog class="base-dialog" @close="$emit('close')">
      <TransitionChild enter="duration-200 ease-out" enter-from="opacity-0" enter-to="opacity-100"
                       leave="duration-150 ease-in" leave-from="opacity-100" leave-to="opacity-0">
        <div class="fixed inset-0 bg-ink/60" aria-hidden="true" />
      </TransitionChild>
      <div class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto">
        <div class="flex min-h-full items-center justify-center p-4">
          <DialogPanel class="relative w-full max-w-xl rounded-[22px] bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl">
            <button type="button" class="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-brand-50"
                    aria-label="Tutup" @click="$emit('close')"><XMarkIcon class="size-5" /></button>
            <p v-if="eyebrow" class="eyebrow">{{ eyebrow }}</p>
            <DialogTitle as="h2" class="font-display text-2xl mb-4 pr-8">{{ title }}</DialogTitle>
            <slot />
          </DialogPanel>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  </TransitionRoot>
</template>

=== src/components/ContactSection.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { ArrowUpRightIcon, DocumentDuplicateIcon, CheckIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import MapCard from './MapCard.vue'
import { CONTACT_ITEMS } from '../data/content'

const items = CONTACT_ITEMS.filter((c) => c.icon !== 'pin')
const canCopy = (c) => c.icon === 'whatsapp' || c.icon === 'mail'
const openText = (c) => ({ whatsapp: 'Chat WhatsApp', instagram: 'Buka Instagram', facebook: 'Buka Facebook', youtube: 'Buka YouTube', mail: 'Kirim email' }[c.icon] || 'Buka')

const digits = (v) => String(v).replace(/\D/g, '')
// +6281298850904 -> +62 812-9885-0904 (hanya tampilan)
function display(c) {
  if (c.icon !== 'whatsapp') return c.value
  const d = digits(c.value)
  return d.startsWith('62') && d.length >= 10 ? `+62 ${d.slice(2, 5)}-${d.slice(5, 9)}-${d.slice(9)}` : c.value
}
const copyText = (c) => (c.icon === 'whatsapp' ? '+' + digits(c.value) : c.value)

const copied = ref('')
let timer
async function copy(c) {
  const text = copyText(c)
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
  } catch {
    const t = document.createElement('textarea')
    t.value = text; t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;opacity:0'
    document.body.appendChild(t); t.select()
    try { document.execCommand('copy') } catch { /* diabaikan */ }
    t.remove()
  }
  copied.value = c.href
  clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => (copied.value = ''), 2000)
}
</script>

<template>
  <section id="kontak" class="section ct" aria-labelledby="h-kontak">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="ct-head">
        <p class="eyebrow">Kontak</p>
        <h2 id="h-kontak">Hubungi pengurus masjid</h2>
        <p class="ct-lead">Silakan hubungi pengurus atau koordinator program sesuai keperluan Anda.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="ct-layout">
        <ul class="ct-list">
          <li v-for="c in items" :key="c.href + c.label" class="ct-row">
            <span class="ct-icon"><svg class="icon" aria-hidden="true"><use :href="'#i-' + c.icon" /></svg></span>
            <div class="ct-text">
              <span class="ct-label">{{ c.label }}</span>
              <strong class="ct-value">{{ display(c) }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="ct-tools">
              <a class="ct-tool" :href="c.href" target="_blank" rel="noopener" :title="openText(c)" :aria-label="openText(c) + ': ' + c.label">
                <ArrowUpRightIcon class="size-4" />
              </a>
              <button v-if="canCopy(c)" type="button" class="ct-tool" :class="{ 'is-done': copied === c.href }"
                      :title="copied === c.href ? 'Tersalin' : 'Salin'" :aria-label="'Salin ' + c.label" @click="copy(c)">
                <CheckIcon v-if="copied === c.href" class="size-4" /><DocumentDuplicateIcon v-else class="size-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </li>
        </ul>
        <MapCard />
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

=== src/components/DonationDialog.vue ===
<script setup>
import { reactive, computed, watch, ref } from 'vue'
import { CheckCircleIcon, DocumentDuplicateIcon, CheckIcon, ArrowDownTrayIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import BaseDialog from './BaseDialog.vue'
import { PROGRAMS, PAYMENT } from '../data/content'
import { supabase, submitDonation, refreshDonations } from '../admin/db'
import { store } from '../store'

const copied = ref(false)
let copyTimer
async function copyNo() {
  const text = String(chosen.value.nomor || '').replace(/\D/g, '') // angka saja, tanpa spasi/strip
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text) }
  catch {
    const t = document.createElement('textarea')
    t.value = text; t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;opacity:0'
    document.body.appendChild(t); t.select()
    try { document.execCommand('copy') } catch { /* diabaikan */ }
    t.remove()
  }
  copied.value = true
  clearTimeout(copyTimer); copyTimer = setTimeout(() => (copied.value = false), 2000)
}

async function saveQris() {
  const src = chosen.value.gambar?.src
  if (!src) return
  const file = `QRIS-${String(chosen.value.nama || 'masjid').replace(/[^\w-]+/g, '-')}.jpg`
  try {
    const blob = await (await fetch(src)).blob()
    const a = document.createElement('a')
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = file
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove()
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000)
  } catch { window.open(src, '_blank', 'noopener') } // fallback bila diblokir
}

const ANON = 'Hamba Allah'

// Metode pembayaran mengikuti Admin > Donasi > Pembayaran: yang kosong tidak ditampilkan.
const methods = ref([])
const buildMethods = () => PAYMENT.metode.map((m) => ({ ...m, label: m.tipe === 'bank' ? 'Transfer ' + m.nama : m.nama }))
const chosen = ref(null)

const form = reactive({ name: '', method: '', anon: false })
const errors = reactive({ name: '' })
const step = ref('form') // form | pay | sending | done
const draft = ref(null)
const failed = ref('')
const open = computed(() => store.donationProgram !== null)

// Program mengikuti kartu yang ditekan, tidak bisa diganti di dalam form.
const program = computed(() => PROGRAMS.find((p) => p.id === store.donationProgram) || PROGRAMS[0] || null)

let savedName = ''
watch(() => form.anon, (on) => {
  if (on) { savedName = form.name === ANON ? '' : form.name; form.name = ANON; errors.name = '' }
  else form.name = savedName
})

watch(() => store.donationProgram, (id) => {
  if (id === null) return
  methods.value = buildMethods()
  savedName = ''
  Object.assign(form, { name: '', method: methods.value[0]?.id || '', anon: false })
  errors.name = ''
  step.value = 'form'; failed.value = ''; draft.value = null
})

const adminWa = () => `https://wa.me/${PAYMENT.wa}`
const waLink = (d) => `https://wa.me/${PAYMENT.wa}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
  `Assalamu'alaikum, saya ${d.name} sudah melakukan donasi untuk program ${program.value?.name || ''} via ${d.method}. Mohon dicek ya, terima kasih.`)}`

function toPayment() {
  if (!form.anon && form.name.trim().length < 3) { errors.name = 'Isi nama minimal 3 karakter.'; return document.getElementById('donorName')?.focus() }
  errors.name = ''
  chosen.value = methods.value.find((m) => m.id === form.method)
  draft.value = {
    id: `donasi-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    name: form.anon ? ANON : form.name.trim(), program: program.value.id, amount: 0, method: chosen.value.label, anon: form.anon,
  }
  failed.value = ''; step.value = 'pay'
}

async function paid() {
  if (!supabase) { window.open(waLink(draft.value), '_blank', 'noopener'); store.donationProgram = null; return }
  step.value = 'sending'; failed.value = ''
  try {
    await submitDonation(draft.value)
    await refreshDonations().catch(() => {})
    step.value = 'done'
  } catch (e) {
    step.value = 'pay'
    failed.value = /terlalu banyak/.test(e.message) ? e.message : 'Konfirmasi belum tercatat di sistem. Coba lagi, atau kabari pengurus lewat WhatsApp.'
  }
}

const stepIdx = computed(() => ({ form: 0, pay: 1, sending: 1, done: 2 }[step.value]))
const title = computed(() => ({ form: 'Donasi', pay: 'Selesaikan pembayaran', sending: 'Mencatat konfirmasi…', done: 'Konfirmasi diterima' }[step.value]))
</script>

<template>
  <BaseDialog :open="open" :title="title" eyebrow="Donasi" @close="store.donationProgram = null">
    <ol class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs mb-5" aria-label="Langkah donasi">
      <li v-for="(l, i) in ['Data', 'Pembayaran', 'Selesai']" :key="l" class="flex items-center gap-2" :class="i <= stepIdx ? 'text-brand-700 font-semibold' : 'text-[#7f918b]'" :aria-current="i === stepIdx ? 'step' : undefined">
        <span class="grid size-6 place-items-center rounded-full text-[11px]" :class="i <= stepIdx ? 'bg-brand-700 text-white' : 'bg-brand-50'">{{ i + 1 }}</span>{{ l }}
        <span v-if="i < 2" class="mx-1 h-px w-6 bg-[#c9d9d1]" aria-hidden="true"></span>
      </li>
    </ol>

    <!-- 1. Isi data -->
    <form v-if="step === 'form'" novalidate @submit.prevent="toPayment">
      <p v-if="program" class="mb-4 text-sm">Donasi untuk <b>{{ program.name }}</b></p>

      <div class="field" :class="{ 'has-error': errors.name }">
        <label for="donorName">Nama</label>
        <input id="donorName" v-model="form.name" type="text" autocomplete="name" maxlength="80" placeholder="Nama lengkap" :disabled="form.anon" @input="errors.name = ''">
        <p class="err" role="alert">{{ errors.name }}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="field"><label class="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium" style="flex-direction:row"><input v-model="form.anon" type="checkbox" style="width:18px;height:18px;padding:0;flex:none"> Donasi sebagai “Hamba Allah”</label></div>

      <template v-if="methods.length">
        <div class="field">
          <label for="donorMethod">Metode pembayaran</label>
          <select id="donorMethod" v-model="form.method"><option v-for="m in methods" :key="m.id" :value="m.id">{{ m.label }}</option></select>
        </div>
        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">Lanjut ke pembayaran</button>
      </template>
      <div v-else class="rounded-xl bg-brand-50 p-4 text-sm">
        <p class="mb-3">Metode pembayaran belum tersedia. Silakan hubungi pengurus untuk berdonasi.</p>
        <a class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" :href="adminWa()" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Hubungi pengurus (WhatsApp)</a>
      </div>
    </form>

    <!-- 2. Pembayaran -->
    <div v-else-if="step === 'pay' || step === 'sending'">
      <p class="mb-4">Donasi untuk <b>{{ program?.name }}</b> via <b>{{ draft.method }}</b>.</p>

      <div class="rounded-xl bg-brand-50 p-4 mb-4 text-sm">
        <template v-if="chosen.tipe === 'qris'">
          <p class="font-semibold mb-2">Scan {{ chosen.nama }}</p>
          <img :src="chosen.gambar?.src" :alt="chosen.nama" class="w-full max-w-xs mx-auto rounded-lg bg-white" loading="lazy">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm mt-3" @click="saveQris">
            <ArrowDownTrayIcon class="size-4" /> Unduh QRIS
          </button>
        </template>
        <template v-else>
          <p class="font-semibold mb-1">{{ chosen.tipe === 'bank' ? 'Transfer ke rekening ' : 'Kirim ke ' }}{{ chosen.nama }}</p>
          <p class="text-lg font-bold">{{ chosen.nomor }}</p>
          <p v-if="chosen.pemilik">a.n. {{ chosen.pemilik }}</p>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm mt-3" @click="copyNo">
            <CheckIcon v-if="copied" class="size-4" /><DocumentDuplicateIcon v-else class="size-4" />
            {{ copied ? 'Tersalin' : (chosen.tipe === 'bank' ? 'Salin nomor rekening' : 'Salin nomor') }}
          </button>
        </template>
      </div>

      <p class="text-xs mb-3 text-[#5f736d]">Setelah membayar, tekan “Saya sudah bayar”. Pengurus akan mengecek pembayaran Anda.</p>
      <p v-if="failed" class="mb-3 text-sm text-red-700" role="alert">{{ failed }}</p>
      <div class="flex gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" :disabled="step === 'sending'" @click="step = 'form'">Kembali</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" :disabled="step === 'sending'" @click="paid">{{ step === 'sending' ? 'Mencatat…' : 'Saya sudah bayar' }}</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 3. Selesai -->
    <div v-else-if="step === 'done'">
      <div class="text-center mb-5">
        <CheckCircleIcon class="size-14 mx-auto text-brand-700" />
        <p class="mt-2">Terima kasih! Konfirmasi Anda sudah kami terima dan sedang dicek oleh pengurus.</p>
      </div>
      <p class="text-sm text-[#5f736d] mb-5 text-center">Ingin mempercepat? Anda bisa mengabari pengurus lewat WhatsApp (opsional).</p>
      <div class="flex gap-3 flex-wrap justify-center">
        <a class="btn btn-secondary" :href="waLink(draft)" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Kirim ke admin (WhatsApp)</a>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" @click="store.donationProgram = null">Tutup</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </BaseDialog>
</template>


=== src/components/MapCard.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed } from 'vue'
import { ArrowUpRightIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import { PROFILE } from '../data/content'

// Pakai koordinat dari Admin > Kontak bila diisi; kalau belum, Google mencari lewat nama + alamat masjid.
const src = computed(() => {
  const q = PROFILE.hasCoords ? `${PROFILE.lat},${PROFILE.lng}` : `Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah ${PROFILE.alamat}`
  return `https://maps.google.com/maps?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&z=16&output=embed`
})
// "Jl. X, Babakan, Kec. Setu, Kota Tangerang Selatan, Banten" -> "Babakan, Kec. Setu, Tangerang Selatan"
const area = computed(() => {
  const parts = PROFILE.alamat.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
  return parts.length > 3 ? parts.slice(1, 4).join(', ').replace('Kota ', '') : PROFILE.alamat
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="map-card">
    <div class="map-frame">
      <iframe :src="src" title="Peta lokasi Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer-when-downgrade" allowfullscreen></iframe>
    </div>
    <div class="map-info">
      <div>
        <span class="map-label">Titik lokasi</span>
        <strong>{{ area }}</strong>
      </div>
      <a class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" :href="PROFILE.maps" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
        Buka di Google Maps <ArrowUpRightIcon class="size-4" />
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

=== src/components/ProgramChart.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, watch, onMounted, onBeforeUnmount } from 'vue'
import { Chart, BarController, BarElement, CategoryScale, LinearScale, Tooltip } from 'chart.js'
import { DONORS, rupiah } from '../data/content'
import { live } from '../admin/db'

Chart.register(BarController, BarElement, CategoryScale, LinearScale, Tooltip)
const props = defineProps({ id: String })
const el = ref(null)
const empty = ref(false)
let chart

// Total donasi terverifikasi per bulan (6 bulan terakhir) untuk SATU program saja
function monthly() {
  const m = {}
  DONORS.filter((d) => d.program === props.id).forEach((d) => { const k = d.date.slice(0, 7); m[k] = (m[k] || 0) + d.amount })
  const keys = Object.keys(m).sort().slice(-6)
  return {
    labels: keys.map((k) => new Date(k + '-01T00:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { month: 'short', year: '2-digit', timeZone: 'UTC' })),
    data: keys.map((k) => m[k]),
  }
}
onMounted(() => {
  const { labels, data } = monthly(); empty.value = !data.length
  chart = new Chart(el.value, {
    type: 'bar',
    data: { labels, datasets: [{ data, backgroundColor: '#146b58', borderRadius: 6 }] },
    options: {
      responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: (c) => rupiah(c.parsed.y) } } },
      scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, suggestedMax: 100000, ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 5, callback: (v) => (v >= 1e6 ? v / 1e6 + ' jt' : v >= 1e3 ? v / 1e3 + ' rb' : v) } } },
    },
  })
})
watch(live, () => {
  if (!chart) return
  const { labels, data } = monthly(); empty.value = !data.length
  chart.data.labels = labels; chart.data.datasets[0].data = data; chart.update()
})
onBeforeUnmount(() => chart?.destroy())
</script>

<template>
  <div class="relative h-40 mt-4">
    <canvas ref="el" role="img" aria-label="Donasi terverifikasi per bulan untuk program ini"></canvas>
    <p v-if="empty" class="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center text-sm text-[#5f736d]">Belum ada donasi terverifikasi.</p>
  </div>
</template>


=== src/composables/usePrayer.js ===
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue'
import { PRAYERS } from '../data/content'

const minutesOf = (t) => t.split(':').reduce((h, m) => h * 60 + Number(m), 0)

function jakartaSeconds() {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    timeZone: 'Asia/Jakarta', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hour12: false,
  }).formatToParts(new Date())
  const get = (t) => Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === t).value)
  return (get('hour') % 24) * 3600 + get('minute') * 60 + get('second')
}

export function dateText() {
  const opt = { timeZone: 'Asia/Jakarta', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }
  let text = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', { ...opt, weekday: 'long' }).format(new Date())
  try {
    const h = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID-u-ca-islamic-umalqura', opt).format(new Date())
    text += ' · ' + h.replace(/\s*H$/, '') + ' H'
  } catch { /* hijriah tidak didukung */ }
  return text
}

export function usePrayer() {
  const now = ref(jakartaSeconds())
  let timer
  onMounted(() => { timer = setInterval(() => (now.value = jakartaSeconds()), 1000) })
  onUnmounted(() => clearInterval(timer))

  const nextIndex = computed(() => PRAYERS.findIndex((p) => minutesOf(p.time) * 60 > now.value))
  const tomorrow = computed(() => nextIndex.value === -1)
  const next = computed(() => PRAYERS[tomorrow.value ? 0 : nextIndex.value])
  const left = computed(() => {
    const target = minutesOf(next.value.time) * 60 + (tomorrow.value ? 86400 : 0)
    const d = target - now.value
    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
    return { h: pad(Math.floor(d / 3600)), m: pad(Math.floor((d % 3600) / 60)), s: pad(d % 60) }
  })
  const state = (i) => {
    const n = tomorrow.value ? 0 : nextIndex.value
    return { 'is-next': i === n, 'is-past': tomorrow.value ? i !== n : i < n }
  }
  return { next, left, state, date: dateText() }
}

export function esc(value) {
    return String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (character) {
      return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[character];
    });
  }

export function icon(name) {
    return '<svg class="icon" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-' + name + '"/></svg>';
  }

export const patternIcon = '<svg class="icon" style="--icon-size:46px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-mosque"/></svg>';
export const readerPatternIcon = '<svg class="icon" style="--icon-size:54px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-mosque"/></svg>';

export function parseISODate(iso) {
    const parts = iso.split("-").map(Number);
    return new Date(Date.UTC(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2]));
  }

export function longDate(iso) {
    const date = parseISODate(iso);
    return date.getUTCDate() + " " + MONTHS_LONG[date.getUTCMonth()] + " " + date.getUTCFullYear();
  }

export function shortDate(iso) {
    const date = parseISODate(iso);
    return date.getUTCDate() + " " + MONTHS_SHORT[date.getUTCMonth()] + " " + date.getUTCFullYear();
  }

export function weekday(iso) {
    return DAYS_LONG[parseISODate(iso).getUTCDay()];
  }

export function programName(id) {
    const program = PROGRAMS.find((item) => item.id === id);
    return program ? program.name : id;
  }

export function mediaBlock(image, className) {
    if (image) {
      return '<div class="' + className + '"><img src="' + esc(image.src) + '" alt="' + esc(image.alt) + '" width="' + image.width + '" height="' + image.height + '" loading="lazy"></div>';
    }
    return '<div class="' + className + ' media-pattern" aria-hidden="true">' + patternIcon + "</div>";
  }


export function jakartaISO() {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Jakarta', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(new Date())
  const get = (t) => parts.find((p) => p.type === t).value
  return `${get('year')}-${get('month')}-${get('day')}`
}
export const TODAY_ISO = jakartaISO()

/** Tautan WhatsApp untuk mendaftar ke program. Nomor program dipakai bila diisi, kalau tidak pakai nomor admin dari Kontak. */
export function daftarLink(p) {
  let n = String(p.wa || '').replace(/\D/g, '')
  if (n.startsWith('0')) n = '62' + n.slice(1)
  if (!n) n = String(CONTACT_ITEMS.find((c) => c.icon === 'whatsapp')?.href || '').replace(/\D/g, '')
  const text = `Assalamu'alaikum, saya ingin mendaftar program ${p.title} di Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah.`
  return `https://wa.me/${n}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`
}
