=== src/store.js ===
import { reactive, watch, nextTick } from 'vue'
import { PAGE_TITLES } from './data/content'

const VIEWS = ['beranda', 'profil', 'donasi', 'program', 'berita', 'admin']
// Hash: #beranda, #berita, #berita/<id-artikel>, #kegiatan/<id-kegiatan>
function parse() {
  const [v, id] = location.hash.slice(1).split('/')
  return { view: VIEWS.includes(v) ? v : 'beranda', detail: id || null }
}

export const store = reactive({
  ...parse(),
  donationProgram: null,  // null = tertutup, '' = terbuka tanpa program
  go(name, detail = null) { this.view = name; this.detail = detail; window.scrollTo({ top: 0 }) },
  goContact() {
    this.go('beranda')
    nextTick(() => document.getElementById('kontak')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' }))
  },
})

watch(() => [store.view, store.detail], ([v, d]) => {
  history.replaceState(null, '', '#' + v + (d ? '/' + d : ''))
  document.title = PAGE_TITLES[v === 'berita' ? 'artikel' : v] || "Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah"
}, { immediate: true })
window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => Object.assign(store, parse()))


=== src/utils/receipt.js ===
import { rupiah, longDate } from '../data/content'

function round(g, x, y, w, h, r) {
  g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x + r, y)
  g.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r)
  g.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); g.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); g.closePath()
}
function fit(g, text, max) {
  let t = String(text)
  while (t.length > 1 && g.measureText(t).width > max) t = t.slice(0, -2)
  return t === String(text) ? t : t.trimEnd() + '…'
}

/** Buat bukti donasi sebagai gambar PNG dan unduh. d = baris donasi dari server. */
export function downloadReceipt(d, programName) {
  const W = 760, H = 960, font = "'Plus Jakarta Sans', system-ui, sans-serif"
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H
  const g = c.getContext('2d')
  g.fillStyle = '#e2f1ea'; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H)
  g.fillStyle = '#fff'; round(g, 40, 40, W - 80, H - 80, 28); g.fill()
  g.fillStyle = '#146b58'; round(g, 40, 40, W - 80, 170, 28); g.fill(); g.fillRect(40, 150, W - 80, 60)
  g.textAlign = 'center'
  g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.font = `700 30px ${font}`; g.fillText("Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah", W / 2, 110)
  g.fillStyle = '#a8d6c6'; g.font = `600 22px ${font}`; g.fillText('BUKTI DONASI', W / 2, 152)

  g.fillStyle = '#0b4036'; g.font = `700 56px ${font}`; g.fillText(rupiah(d.amount), W / 2, 295)
  const ok = d.status === 'verified'
  g.fillStyle = ok ? '#146b58' : '#b45309'; g.font = `700 20px ${font}`
  g.fillText(ok ? '✓ TERVERIFIKASI' : 'MENUNGGU VERIFIKASI PENGURUS', W / 2, 340)

  const rows = [['No. bukti', d.id.replace('donasi-', '').toUpperCase()], ['Tanggal', longDate(d.date)], ['Nama', d.name],
    ['Program', programName], ['Metode', d.method]]
  rows.forEach(([k, v], i) => {
    const y = 420 + i * 66
    g.strokeStyle = '#dce8e2'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(90, y - 34); g.lineTo(W - 90, y - 34); g.stroke()
    g.textAlign = 'left'; g.fillStyle = '#5f736d'; g.font = `500 20px ${font}`; g.fillText(k, 90, y)
    g.textAlign = 'right'; g.fillStyle = '#0e2b25'; g.font = `600 22px ${font}`; g.fillText(fit(g, v, 400), W - 90, y)
  })
  g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillStyle = '#0e2b25'; g.font = `600 22px ${font}`
  g.fillText('Terima kasih atas donasi Anda.', W / 2, 810)
  g.fillStyle = '#5f736d'; g.font = `500 19px ${font}`
  g.fillText('Semoga menjadi amal jariyah dan keberkahan bagi keluarga.', W / 2, 846)
  g.font = `500 16px ${font}`; g.fillText('Bukti ini dibuat otomatis oleh sistem donasi masjid.', W / 2, 890)

  c.toBlob((b) => {
    const a = document.createElement('a')
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(b); a.download = `bukti-donasi-${d.id.replace('donasi-', '')}.png`
    a.click(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000)
  }, 'image/png')
}


=== src/views/DonationView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'
import Media from '../components/Media.vue'
import { PROGRAMS, DONORS, rupiah, shortDate, longDate, programName } from '../data/content'
import { store } from '../store'
import { live } from '../admin/db'
import BackHome from '../components/BackHome.vue'

// `live` naik tiap kali data donasi berubah (realtime), supaya angka ikut dihitung ulang
const programs = computed(() => (live.value, PROGRAMS.slice()))
const percent = (p) => Math.min(100, Math.round((p.collected / p.target) * 100))

// 10 donatur terakhir (riwayat transaksi lengkap hanya ada di admin), bisa difilter per program
const filter = ref('all')
const lastDonors = computed(() => (live.value, [...DONORS]
  .filter((d) => filter.value === 'all' || d.program === filter.value)
  .sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
  .slice(0, 10)))
</script>

<template>
  <section class="view don" aria-label="Donasi">
    <div class="don-hero">
      <div class="wrap">
        <BackHome />
        <h1>Menyalurkan kebaikan dengan amanah</h1>
        <p class="don-lead">Pilih program yang ingin Anda dukung, selesaikan pembayaran lewat transfer bank atau QRIS, dan pantau pembaruan donasi secara otomatis.</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="don-body">
      <div class="wrap">
        <p v-if="!programs.length" class="page-lead" style="margin-top:32px">Belum ada program donasi yang aktif.</p>
        <div v-else class="don-programs">
          <article v-for="p in programs" :key="p.id" class="don-card">
            <Media :image="p.image" cls="don-media" />
            <div class="don-card-body">
              <span class="don-kicker">{{ p.kicker }}</span>
              <h3>{{ p.name }}</h3>
              <p class="program-desc">{{ p.desc }}</p>
              <ul class="allocation-list"><li v-for="u in p.uses" :key="u">{{ u }}</li></ul>

              <div class="don-stat">
                <template v-if="p.target">
                  <div class="don-stat-row">
                    <div><span class="don-lbl">Terkumpul</span><strong>{{ rupiah(p.collected) }}</strong></div>
                    <div class="right"><span class="don-lbl">Target</span><strong>{{ rupiah(p.target) }}</strong></div>
                  </div>
                  <div class="progress" role="progressbar" :aria-valuenow="percent(p)" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" :aria-label="'Progres ' + p.name">
                    <span :style="{ width: percent(p) + '%' }"></span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="don-stat-foot">
                    <b>{{ percent(p) }}% tercapai</b>
                    <span v-if="p.deadline">Batas donasi: {{ longDate(p.deadline) }}</span>
                  </div>
                </template>
                <template v-else>
                  <span class="don-lbl">Terkumpul</span><strong>{{ rupiah(p.collected) }}</strong>
                </template>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="don-foot">
              <span>{{ p.donors }} donatur</span>
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @click="store.donationProgram = p.id">Donasi sekarang</button>
            </div>
          </article>
        </div>

        <section class="don-recent" aria-labelledby="h-donatur">
          <div class="don-recent-head">
            <div>
              <h2 id="h-donatur">10 donatur terakhir</h2>
              <p>Catatan donasi terbaru yang masuk dan terverifikasi secara berkala.</p>
            </div>
            <select v-model="filter" class="rounded-xl border border-[#c9d9d1] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium" aria-label="Filter program">
              <option value="all">Semua program</option>
              <option v-for="p in programs" :key="p.id" :value="p.id">{{ p.name }}</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="table-card">
            <table class="data-table">
              <thead><tr><th>Donatur</th><th>Program</th><th>Tanggal</th><th class="num">Nominal</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr v-for="(d, i) in lastDonors" :key="d.id || i">
                  <td data-label="Donatur"><strong>{{ d.name }}</strong></td>
                  <td data-label="Program"><span class="don-pill">{{ programName(d.program) }}</span></td>
                  <td data-label="Tanggal">{{ shortDate(d.date) }}</td>
                  <td data-label="Nominal" class="num">{{ rupiah(d.amount) }}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr v-if="!lastDonors.length"><td colspan="4">Belum ada donasi terverifikasi.</td></tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>


=== src/views/HomeView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, onBeforeUnmount } from 'vue'
const pages = ref(1)
const active = ref(0)
function measure() {
  const t = track.value
  if (!t) return
  const max = t.scrollWidth - t.clientWidth
  pages.value = max > 4 ? Math.ceil(t.scrollWidth / t.clientWidth - 0.01) : 1
  active.value = max > 4 ? Math.round((t.scrollLeft / max) * (pages.value - 1)) : 0
}
function toPage(i) {
  const t = track.value
  const max = t.scrollWidth - t.clientWidth
  t.scrollTo({ left: pages.value > 1 ? (max * i) / (pages.value - 1) : 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
}
let ro
onMounted(() => { measure(); ro = new ResizeObserver(measure); ro.observe(track.value) })
onBeforeUnmount(() => ro?.disconnect())
import { ArrowRightIcon, ChevronLeftIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import PrayerCard from '../components/PrayerCard.vue'
import ArticleCard from '../components/ArticleCard.vue'
import ContactSection from '../components/ContactSection.vue'
import Media from '../components/Media.vue'
import { ACTIVITIES, ARTICLES } from '../data/content'
import { store } from '../store'

const articles = [...ARTICLES].sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date)).slice(0, 3)
const programs = [...ACTIVITIES]

const track = ref(null)
function slide(dir) {
  const t = track.value
  if (!t) return
  const max = t.scrollWidth - t.clientWidth
  if (dir > 0 && t.scrollLeft >= max - 4) t.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
  else if (dir < 0 && t.scrollLeft <= 4) t.scrollTo({ left: max, behavior: 'smooth' })
  else t.scrollBy({ left: dir * t.clientWidth * 0.8, behavior: 'smooth' })
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="view" aria-label="Beranda">
    <div class="hero">
      <div class="wrap hero-grid">
        <div class="hero-profile">

               alt="Masjid dengan kubah putih dan menara berkisi, tampak dari halaman luas.">
          <div class="hero-profile-scrim" aria-hidden="true"></div>
          <div class="hero-copy">
            <p class="eyebrow eyebrow-light">Profil singkat</p>
            <h1>Masjid Jami'<br>Al-Barokah</h1>
            <p class="hero-tagline">Pusat ibadah, ilmu, dan kepedulian warga Tangerang Selatan. Mari sholat berjamaah, menuntut ilmu, dan berbagi kebaikan bersama.</p>
            <div class="hero-actions">
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-light" @click="store.go('profil')">Lihat profil <ArrowRightIcon class="size-4 inline" /></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="prayer-wrap"><PrayerCard /></div>

        </div>

                    :aria-label="`Slide ${n} dari ${pages}`" :aria-current="n - 1 === active ? 'true' : undefined" @click="toPage(n - 1)"></button>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="pcar-btn pcar-next" aria-label="Program berikutnya" @click="slide(1)"><ChevronRightIcon class="size-5" /></button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="section section-alt" aria-labelledby="h-berita">
      <div class="wrap">
        <div class="section-head">
          <div><p class="eyebrow">Berita</p><h2 id="h-berita">Kabar dan artikel terbaru</h2></div>

        </div>

      </div>

  </section>

=== src/views/NewsView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import DOMPurify from 'dompurify'
import { ArrowLeftIcon, MagnifyingGlassIcon, ChevronLeftIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import ArticleCard from '../components/ArticleCard.vue'
import BackHome from '../components/BackHome.vue'
import Media from '../components/Media.vue'
import { ARTICLES, longDate } from '../data/content'
import { store } from '../store'

const PER_PAGE = 6
const sorted = [...ARTICLES].sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
const categories = [...new Set(ARTICLES.map((a) => a.category).filter(Boolean))].sort()
const item = computed(() => (store.detail ? ARTICLES.find((a) => a.id === store.detail) : null))
const body = computed(() => (item.value ? DOMPurify.sanitize(item.value.body || '') : ''))

const q = ref('')
const cat = ref('')
const page = ref(1)
const filtered = computed(() => {
  const s = q.value.trim().toLowerCase()
  return sorted.filter((a) => (!cat.value || a.category === cat.value)
    && (!s || [a.title, a.excerpt, a.author].some((t) => String(t || '').toLowerCase().includes(s))))
})
const pages = computed(() => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.value.length / PER_PAGE)))
const shown = computed(() => filtered.value.slice((page.value - 1) * PER_PAGE, page.value * PER_PAGE))
watch([q, cat], () => { page.value = 1 })

const listEl = ref(null)
function setPage(n) {
  page.value = Math.min(pages.value, Math.max(1, n))
  listEl.value?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="view" aria-label="Berita">
    <template v-if="item">
      <div class="wrap section-tight">
        <article class="reader">
          <button type="button" class="text-link reader-back" @click="store.go('berita')"><ArrowLeftIcon class="size-4 inline" /> Kembali ke berita</button>
          <p class="eyebrow">{{ item.category }}</p>
          <h1>{{ item.title }}</h1>
          <p class="reader-meta">{{ longDate(item.date) }}<template v-if="item.read"> · {{ item.read }} menit baca</template> · Penulis: {{ item.author }}</p>
          <Media :image="item.image" cls="reader-media" />
          <div class="prose" v-html="body"></div>
          <div class="reader-footer">
            <p>Disusun oleh <strong>Sekretariat Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah</strong> untuk jamaah dan warga sekitar.</p>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" @click="store.go('berita')">Lihat berita lain</button>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>
    </template>
    <template v-else>
      <div class="page-intro"><div class="wrap">
        <BackHome />
        <h1>Berita &amp; artikel</h1>
        <p class="page-lead">Kabar kegiatan, kajian singkat, dan tulisan untuk jamaah.</p>
      </div></div>
      <div class="wrap section-tight">
        <div ref="listEl" class="news-tools">
          <label class="news-search">
            <span class="sr-only">Cari berita</span>
            <MagnifyingGlassIcon aria-hidden="true" />
            <input v-model="q" type="search" placeholder="Cari judul, ringkasan, atau penulis…">
          </label>
          <label>
            <span class="sr-only">Kategori</span>
            <select v-model="cat" class="news-select">
              <option value="">Semua kategori</option>
              <option v-for="c in categories" :key="c" :value="c">{{ c }}</option>
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>

        <p v-if="!filtered.length" class="page-lead">Tidak ada berita yang cocok dengan pencarian Anda.</p>
        <div v-else class="article-grid"><ArticleCard v-for="a in shown" :key="a.id" :article="a" /></div>

        <nav v-if="pages > 1" class="pager" aria-label="Halaman berita">
          <button type="button" aria-label="Halaman sebelumnya" :disabled="page === 1" @click="setPage(page - 1)"><ChevronLeftIcon class="size-4" /></button>
          <button v-for="n in pages" :key="n" type="button" :class="{ 'is-active': n === page }" :aria-current="n === page ? 'page' : undefined" @click="setPage(n)">{{ n }}</button>
          <button type="button" aria-label="Halaman berikutnya" :disabled="page === pages" @click="setPage(page + 1)"><ChevronRightIcon class="size-4" /></button>
        </nav>
      </div>
    </template>
  </section>
</template>

=== src/views/ProfilView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onBeforeUnmount } from 'vue'
import { Dialog, DialogPanel, DialogTitle } from '@headlessui/vue'
import { ChevronLeftIcon, ChevronRightIcon, XMarkIcon, ArrowTopRightOnSquareIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import { GALLERY, PROFILE } from '../data/content'
import { store } from '../store'
import BackHome from '../components/BackHome.vue'

const FALLBACK = '/assets/stock/masjid-hero.jpg'
const onErr = (e) => { e.target.onerror = null; e.target.src = FALLBACK }

const items = computed(() => GALLERY)
const total = computed(() => GALLERY.length)
const idx = ref(0)
const lb = ref(false)
const lbIdx = ref(0)
const wrap = (i) => (total.value ? ((i % total.value) + total.value) % total.value : 0)
const go = (i) => { idx.value = wrap(i) }
const openLb = (i) => { lbIdx.value = wrap(i); lb.value = true }

// Peta: koordinat dari Admin > Kontak bila ada, kalau tidak Google mencari lewat nama + alamat
const mapSrc = computed(() => {
  const q = PROFILE.hasCoords ? `${PROFILE.lat},${PROFILE.lng}` : `Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah ${PROFILE.alamat}`
  return `https://maps.google.com/maps?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&z=17&output=embed`
})

// geser (swipe) di HP
let sx = 0, sy = 0, swiped = false
const onTouchStart = (e) => { sx = e.changedTouches[0].screenX; sy = e.changedTouches[0].screenY; swiped = false }
const onTouchEnd = (e) => {
  const dx = e.changedTouches[0].screenX - sx, dy = e.changedTouches[0].screenY - sy
  if (Math.abs(dx) > 40 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) { swiped = true; go(idx.value + (dx < 0 ? 1 : -1)) }
}
const onSliderClick = () => { if (swiped) { swiped = false; return } openLb(idx.value) }

const onKey = (e) => {
  if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(e.target.tagName)) return
  const d = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0
  if (!d) return
  if (lb.value) lbIdx.value = wrap(lbIdx.value + d)
  else if (store.donationProgram === null) go(idx.value + d)
}
onMounted(() => window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey))
onBeforeUnmount(() => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey))
</script>

<template>
  <div class="pf">
    <!-- Hero -->

        <div class="pf-head">
          <p class="pf-eyebrow pf-eyebrow-green">GALERI &amp; FASILITAS</p>
          <h2 id="pf-gal-h" class="pf-h2">Fasilitas &amp; Area Masjid</h2>
        </div>

        <div class="pf-slider" role="region" aria-roledescription="carousel" aria-label="Galeri fasilitas dan area masjid"
             @touchstart.passive="onTouchStart" @touchend.passive="onTouchEnd" @click="onSliderClick">
          <div class="pf-track" :style="{ transform: `translateX(-${idx * 100}%)` }">
            <div v-for="g in items" :key="g.src" class="pf-slide"><img :src="g.src" :alt="g.alt || g.caption" loading="lazy" @error="onErr"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="pf-caption">
            <h3>{{ items[idx]?.caption }}</h3>
            <span class="pf-counter">{{ idx + 1 }} / {{ total }}</span>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="pf-arrow pf-prev" aria-label="Foto sebelumnya" @click.stop="go(idx - 1)"><ChevronLeftIcon /></button>
          <button type="button" class="pf-arrow pf-next" aria-label="Foto berikutnya" @click.stop="go(idx + 1)"><ChevronRightIcon /></button>
        </div>

        <div class="pf-dots" aria-label="Pilih slide galeri">
          <button v-for="(g, i) in items" :key="g.src" type="button" :aria-label="`Tampilkan ${g.caption} (${i + 1} dari ${total})`" :class="{ on: i === idx }" @click="go(i)"></button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Lokasi -->
    <section class="pf-section pf-last" aria-labelledby="pf-loc-h">
      <div class="pf-wrap">
        <div class="pf-card pf-loccard">
          <div class="pf-loc">
            <div class="pf-locinfo">
              <div>
                <p class="pf-eyebrow pf-eyebrow-green">LOKASI MASJID</p>
                <h2 id="pf-loc-h" class="pf-h2">Alamat Masjid</h2>
                <p class="pf-addr">{{ PROFILE.alamat }}</p>
              </div>
              <a class="pf-btn-outline" :href="PROFILE.maps" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><span>Buka di Google Maps</span><ArrowTopRightOnSquareIcon /></a>
            </div>
            <div class="pf-map"><iframe title="Peta lokasi Masjid Jami' Al-Barokah" :src="mapSrc" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer-when-downgrade"></iframe></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Lightbox -->
    <Dialog :open="lb" class="pf-lb-root" @close="lb = false">
      <div class="pf-lb-backdrop" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="pf-lb-wrap">
        <DialogPanel class="pf-lb-panel">
          <button type="button" class="pf-lb-close" aria-label="Tutup galeri" @click="lb = false"><XMarkIcon /></button>
          <div class="pf-lb-stage">
            <img :src="items[lbIdx]?.src" :alt="items[lbIdx]?.alt" @error="onErr">
            <button type="button" class="pf-arrow pf-prev" aria-label="Foto sebelumnya" @click="lbIdx = wrap(lbIdx - 1)"><ChevronLeftIcon /></button>
            <button type="button" class="pf-arrow pf-next" aria-label="Foto berikutnya" @click="lbIdx = wrap(lbIdx + 1)"><ChevronRightIcon /></button>
          </div>
          <div class="pf-lb-foot">
            <DialogTitle as="h3">{{ items[lbIdx]?.caption }}</DialogTitle>
            <span>{{ lbIdx + 1 }} / {{ total }}</span>
          </div>
        </DialogPanel>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  </div>
</template>

=== src/views/ProgramView.vue ===
<script setup>
import { computed } from 'vue'
import DOMPurify from 'dompurify'
import { ClockIcon, ArrowRightIcon, ArrowLeftIcon } from '@heroicons/vue/24/outline'
import BackHome from '../components/BackHome.vue'
import Media from '../components/Media.vue'
import { ACTIVITIES, PROGRAM_GROUPS, daftarLink } from '../data/content'
import { fmtBody } from '../admin/db'
import { store } from '../store'

const groups = PROGRAM_GROUPS
  .map((g) => ({ ...g, items: ACTIVITIES.filter((a) => a.group === g.id) }))
  .filter((g) => g.items.length)

const item = computed(() => (store.detail ? ACTIVITIES.find((a) => a.id === store.detail) : null))
const groupLabel = computed(() => PROGRAM_GROUPS.find((g) => g.id === item.value?.group)?.eyebrow || 'Program')
const html = computed(() => (item.value ? DOMPurify.sanitize(fmtBody(item.value.detail || item.value.desc || '')) : ''))
</script>

<template>
  <section class="view" aria-label="Program">
    <template v-if="item">
      <div class="wrap section-tight">
        <article class="reader">
          <button type="button" class="text-link reader-back" @click="store.go('program')"><ArrowLeftIcon class="size-4 inline" /> Kembali ke program</button>
          <p class="eyebrow">{{ groupLabel }}</p>
          <h1>{{ item.title }}</h1>
          <p v-if="item.schedule" class="reader-meta">Jadwal: {{ item.schedule }}</p>
          <Media :image="item.image" cls="reader-media" />
          <div class="prose" v-html="html"></div>
          <div v-if="item.daftar" class="prg-cta">
            <a class="btn btn-primary" :href="daftarLink(item)" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
              <svg class="icon" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-whatsapp" /></svg> Daftar via WhatsApp
            </a>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>
    </template>

    <template v-else>
      <div class="page-intro"><div class="wrap">
        <BackHome />
        <h1>Program masjid</h1>
        <p class="page-lead">Pendidikan, kepedulian sosial, dan majelis ilmu yang dijalankan pengurus bersama jamaah.</p>
      </div></div>

      <div class="wrap section-tight prg">
        <p v-if="!groups.length" class="page-lead">Program akan segera ditampilkan.</p>
        <section v-for="g in groups" :key="g.id" class="prg-group" :aria-labelledby="'prg-' + g.id">
          <div class="prg-head">
            <p class="eyebrow">{{ g.eyebrow }}</p>
            <h2 :id="'prg-' + g.id">{{ g.title }}</h2>
          </div>
          <div class="prg-grid">
            <article v-for="p in g.items" :key="p.id" class="prg-card">
              <Media :image="p.image" cls="prg-media" />
              <div class="prg-body">
                <h3>{{ p.title }}</h3>
                <p v-if="p.schedule" class="prg-sched"><ClockIcon class="size-4 inline" /> {{ p.schedule }}</p>
                <p>{{ p.desc }}</p>
                <div class="prg-actions">
                  <a v-if="p.daftar" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" :href="daftarLink(p)" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
                    <svg class="icon" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#i-whatsapp" /></svg> Daftar
                  </a>
                  <button type="button" class="text-link" :aria-label="'Lihat selengkapnya: ' + p.title" @click="store.go('program', p.id)">
                    Lihat selengkapnya <ArrowRightIcon class="size-4 inline" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </article>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </template>
  </section>
</template>

=== supabase/donasi-konfirmasi.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di Supabase: SQL Editor > New query > paste > Run. Aman dijalankan ulang.
--
-- Alur donasi baru: pengunjung membayar, lalu menekan "Saya sudah bayar".
-- Donasi selalu tercatat berstatus "pending"; pengurus mengecek lalu mengisi nominal & menekan Verifikasi di admin.

create or replace function submit_donation(p jsonb) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  v_id     text := p->>'id';
  v_prog   text := p->>'program';
  v_name   text := btrim(coalesce(p->>'name', ''));
  v_anon   boolean := coalesce((p->>'anon')::boolean, false);
  v_ip     text;
  v_hdr    json := nullif(current_setting('request.headers', true), '')::json;
  v_row    jsonb;
begin
  if v_id is null or v_id !~ '^donasi-[a-z0-9-]{3,60}$' then raise exception 'id donasi tidak valid'; end if;
  if v_anon then v_name := 'Hamba Allah'; end if;
  if char_length(v_name) not between 3 and 80 then raise exception 'nama tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(p->>'method', '') not in ('Transfer bank', 'QRIS', 'E-wallet') then raise exception 'metode tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(jsonb_typeof(p->'anon'), 'boolean') <> 'boolean' then raise exception 'data tidak valid'; end if;
  if not donation_program_ok(v_prog) then raise exception 'program tidak tersedia'; end if;

  -- batas spam: maks 200 konfirmasi/jam untuk seluruh situs dan 20/jam per alamat IP
  v_ip := nullif(btrim(coalesce(v_hdr->>'cf-connecting-ip', split_part(coalesce(v_hdr->>'x-forwarded-for', ''), ',', 1))), '');
  if (select count(*) from donors where created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 200 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;
  if v_ip is not null and (select count(*) from donors where ip = v_ip and created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 20 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi dari perangkat ini, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;

  -- nominal diisi pengurus saat verifikasi (0 = belum diisi); status selalu pending
  v_row := jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_id, 'name', v_name, 'program', v_prog, 'amount', 0, 'method', p->>'method',
    'date', to_char(now() at time zone 'Asia/Jakarta', 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
    'status', 'pending', 'anon', v_anon);
  insert into donors (id, data, ip) values (v_id, v_row, v_ip);
  return v_row;
end $$;
grant execute on function submit_donation(jsonb) to anon, authenticated;

-- Hapus sisa pengaturan lama dari Pembayaran
update settings set data = data - 'auto' where key = 'bayar';

-- Hapus 6 donatur contoh. Ini TIDAK mengubah angka "Terkumpul" maupun "jumlah donatur" program.
delete from donors where id in ('donasi-1', 'donasi-2', 'donasi-3', 'donasi-4', 'donasi-5', 'donasi-6');


=== supabase/donasi-otomatis.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di SQL Editor (setelah schema, donasi-publik, media, hardening).
-- Donasi dari situs sekarang lewat fungsi submit_donation(): data tercatat di Supabase dan,
-- bila MODE DEMO aktif, langsung terverifikasi + total program naik otomatis.

alter table donors add column if not exists created_at timestamptz not null default now();
alter table donors add column if not exists ip text;

create or replace function submit_donation(p jsonb) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  v_id     text := p->>'id';
  v_prog   text := p->>'program';
  v_name   text := btrim(coalesce(p->>'name', ''));
  v_amount numeric;
  v_auto   boolean;
  v_status text;
  v_ip     text;
  v_hdr    json := nullif(current_setting('request.headers', true), '')::json;
  v_row    jsonb;
begin
  -- validasi (sama ketatnya dengan hardening.sql)
  if v_id is null or v_id !~ '^donasi-[a-z0-9-]{3,60}$' then raise exception 'id donasi tidak valid'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p->'amount') is distinct from 'number' then raise exception 'nominal tidak valid'; end if;
  v_amount := (p->>'amount')::numeric;
  if v_amount not between 10000 and 100000000 then raise exception 'nominal di luar batas'; end if;
  if char_length(v_name) not between 3 and 80 then raise exception 'nama tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(p->>'method', '') not in ('BSI', 'QRIS', 'E-wallet', 'Tunai', 'Lainnya') then raise exception 'metode tidak valid'; end if;
  if coalesce(jsonb_typeof(p->'anon'), 'boolean') <> 'boolean' then raise exception 'data tidak valid'; end if;
  if not donation_program_ok(v_prog) then raise exception 'program tidak tersedia'; end if;

  -- rate limit: maks 200 donasi/jam untuk seluruh situs dan 20/jam per alamat IP
  v_ip := nullif(btrim(coalesce(v_hdr->>'cf-connecting-ip', split_part(coalesce(v_hdr->>'x-forwarded-for', ''), ',', 1))), '');
  if (select count(*) from donors where created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 200 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;
  if v_ip is not null and (select count(*) from donors where ip = v_ip and created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 20 then
    raise exception 'terlalu banyak donasi dari perangkat ini, coba lagi nanti';
  end if;

  -- MODE DEMO: bila settings "bayar" berisi auto=true, donasi langsung terverifikasi
  select coalesce((data->>'auto')::boolean, false) into v_auto from settings where key = 'bayar';
  v_status := case when coalesce(v_auto, false) then 'verified' else 'pending' end;

  v_row := jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_id, 'name', v_name, 'program', v_prog, 'amount', v_amount, 'method', p->>'method',
    'date', to_char(now() at time zone 'Asia/Jakarta', 'YYYY-MM-DD'),
    'status', v_status, 'anon', coalesce((p->>'anon')::boolean, false));
  insert into donors (id, data, ip) values (v_id, v_row, v_ip);

  if v_status = 'verified' then
    update programs set data = jsonb_set(jsonb_set(data, '{collected}', to_jsonb(coalesce((data->>'collected')::numeric, 0) + v_amount)),
                                         '{donors}', to_jsonb(coalesce((data->>'donors')::int, 0) + 1))
     where id = v_prog;
  end if;
  return v_row;
end $$;
grant execute on function submit_donation(jsonb) to anon, authenticated;

-- Pengunjung tidak lagi boleh menulis langsung ke tabel donors; semua lewat fungsi di atas.
drop policy if exists "public donate" on donors;

-- Nyalakan MODE DEMO. Untuk produksi: matikan lewat Admin > Donasi > Pembayaran, atau jalankan:
--   update settings set data = data || '{"auto": false}' where key = 'bayar';
insert into settings (key, data) values ('bayar', '{"auto": true}')
on conflict (key) do update set data = settings.data || '{"auto": true}'::jsonb;


=== supabase/donasi-publik.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di SQL Editor (setelah schema.sql). Aman dijalankan ulang untuk bagian fungsi.

-- 1) Jumlah donasi yang masih menunggu verifikasi (untuk membatasi spam)
create or replace function pending_donor_count() returns int
language sql security definer set search_path = public as
$$ select count(*)::int from donors where data->>'status' = 'pending' $$;
grant execute on function pending_donor_count() to anon;

-- 2) Pengunjung (anon) boleh MENAMBAH donasi, hanya berstatus "pending" dan hanya bila datanya wajar.
--    Mereka tetap tidak bisa membaca, mengubah, atau menghapus isi tabel donors.
drop policy if exists "public donate" on donors;
create policy "public donate" on donors for insert to anon
with check (
  id like 'donasi-%'
  and data->>'status' = 'pending'
  and (data->>'amount')::numeric between 10000 and 100000000
  and char_length(coalesce(data->>'name', '')) between 3 and 80
  and pending_donor_count() < 300
);

-- 3) Realtime: angka program di situs berubah tanpa refresh; admin melihat donasi baru langsung
do $$ begin
  begin alter publication supabase_realtime add table programs; exception when duplicate_object then null; end;
  begin alter publication supabase_realtime add table donors;   exception when duplicate_object then null; end;
end $$;


=== supabase/hardening.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di SQL Editor (setelah donasi-publik.sql dan media.sql).
-- Memperketat apa yang boleh dikirim pengunjung lewat API, di sisi database.

-- 1) Hanya program yang masih aktif yang boleh menerima donasi.
create or replace function donation_program_ok(pid text) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as
$$ select exists (select 1 from programs where id = pid and data->>'status' is distinct from 'selesai') $$;
grant execute on function donation_program_ok(text) to anon;

-- 2) Aturan donasi dari pengunjung diganti dengan versi yang lebih ketat.
drop policy if exists "public donate" on donors;
create policy "public donate" on donors for insert to anon
with check (
  id ~ '^donasi-[a-z0-9-]{3,60}$'
  and data->>'id' = id
  -- hanya kolom yang dikenal, tidak ada data tambahan/sampah
  and (data - array['id','name','program','amount','method','date','status','anon']) = '{}'::jsonb
  and data->>'status' = 'pending'
  and jsonb_typeof(data->'amount') = 'number'
  and (data->>'amount')::numeric between 10000 and 100000000
  and char_length(coalesce(data->>'name', '')) between 3 and 80
  and donation_program_ok(data->>'program')
  and data->>'method' in ('BSI', 'QRIS', 'E-wallet', 'Tunai', 'Lainnya')
  and coalesce(jsonb_typeof(data->'anon'), 'boolean') = 'boolean'
  -- tanggal harus hari ini (toleransi zona waktu), bukan tanggal acak
  and (data->>'date')::date between current_date - 2 and current_date + 1
  and pending_donor_count() < 300
);

-- 3) Bucket gambar: maksimal 3 MB per file, hanya JPEG/PNG/WebP.
update storage.buckets
   set file_size_limit = 3145728,
       allowed_mime_types = array['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp']
 where id = 'media';


=== supabase/media.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di SQL Editor: tempat penyimpanan gambar (acara, kegiatan, berita, galeri).
-- Bucket bersifat publik (gambar boleh dilihat pengunjung); hanya admin yang boleh unggah/ubah/hapus.
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public) values ('media', 'media', true) on conflict (id) do nothing;

drop policy if exists "media admin insert" on storage.objects;
drop policy if exists "media admin update" on storage.objects;
drop policy if exists "media admin delete" on storage.objects;
create policy "media admin insert" on storage.objects for insert to authenticated with check (bucket_id = 'media');
create policy "media admin update" on storage.objects for update to authenticated using (bucket_id = 'media');
create policy "media admin delete" on storage.objects for delete to authenticated using (bucket_id = 'media');


=== supabase/perbaikan-donatur.sql ===
-- Jalankan SEKALI di Supabase: SQL Editor > New query > paste > Run. Aman dijalankan ulang.
-- Memperbaiki "10 donatur terakhir" yang kosong di situs publik.
-- Tabel donors tetap tertutup untuk pengunjung; nama donatur anonim tetap disamarkan jadi "Hamba Allah".

-- 1) Perkuat view lama (dipakai sebagai cadangan)
create or replace view public_donors with (security_invoker = false) as
  select id, pos,
         case when coalesce((data->>'anon')::boolean, false)
              then jsonb_set(data, '{name}', '"Hamba Allah"') else data end as data
  from donors
  where data->>'status' is distinct from 'pending';
grant select on public_donors to anon, authenticated;

-- 2) Fungsi database yang dipakai situs: hanya donasi terverifikasi, terbaru lebih dulu
create or replace function public_donor_list() returns setof jsonb
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select case when coalesce((data->>'anon')::boolean, false)
              then jsonb_set(data, '{name}', '"Hamba Allah"') else data end
  from donors
  where data->>'status' is distinct from 'pending'
  order by pos desc
  limit 300
$$;
grant execute on function public_donor_list() to anon, authenticated;

-- 3) Cek hasil: kedua angka di bawah harus sama dan lebih dari 0 bila sudah ada donasi terverifikasi
select
  (select count(*) from donors where data->>'status' is distinct from 'pending') as terverifikasi_di_tabel,
  (select count(*) from public_donor_list()) as terbaca_lewat_fungsi;


=== supabase/schema.sql ===
-- Jalankan di Supabase: SQL Editor > New query > paste > Run
-- Satu tabel per modul; isi data disimpan sebagai JSON (kolom data), sama seperti struktur di aplikasi.

create table if not exists programs   (pos bigint generated always as identity, id text primary key, data jsonb not null);
create table if not exists events     (pos bigint generated always as identity, id text primary key, data jsonb not null);
create table if not exists activities (pos bigint generated always as identity, id text primary key, data jsonb not null);
create table if not exists articles   (pos bigint generated always as identity, id text primary key, data jsonb not null);
create table if not exists donors     (pos bigint generated always as identity, id text primary key, data jsonb not null);
create table if not exists settings   (key text primary key, data jsonb not null);

alter table programs   enable row level security;
alter table events     enable row level security;
alter table activities enable row level security;
alter table articles   enable row level security;
alter table donors     enable row level security;
alter table settings   enable row level security;

-- Admin (user yang sudah login) boleh semuanya
create policy "admin all" on programs   for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);
create policy "admin all" on events     for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);
create policy "admin all" on activities for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);
create policy "admin all" on articles   for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);
create policy "admin all" on donors     for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);
create policy "admin all" on settings   for all to authenticated using (true) with check (true);

-- Pengunjung (anon) hanya boleh MEMBACA data yang memang tampil di situs publik
create policy "public read" on programs   for select to anon using (data->>'status' is distinct from 'selesai');
create policy "public read" on events     for select to anon using (true);
create policy "public read" on activities for select to anon using (true);
create policy "public read" on articles   for select to anon using (data->>'status' is distinct from 'draft');
create policy "public read" on settings   for select to anon using (key in ('profil','sholat','bayar','kontak'));

-- Donatur: tabel asli TIDAK dibuka ke publik. Situs publik membaca lewat view ini
-- (hanya donasi terverifikasi, nama anonim disamarkan menjadi "Hamba Allah").
create or replace view public_donors as
  select id, pos,
         case when coalesce((data->>'anon')::boolean, false)
              then jsonb_set(data, '{name}', '"Hamba Allah"') else data end as data
  from donors
  where data->>'status' is distinct from 'pending';
grant select on public_donors to anon, authenticated;


=== supabase/seed.sql ===
